Across synthetic controllable tasks, executable code tasks, real benchmark traces, held-out splits, and a prospective validation batch, EFC-based coordinates consistently predict failure rates better than raw-compute baselines and a strong multivariate SAS baseline. In controlled scaling, raw tokens and tool calls explain limited variation ($R^2=0.33$ and $0.42$), SAS reaches $0.88$, while Oracle-EFC and Estimated-EFC reach $0.94$ and Oracle-EFC/$D_{\mathrm{task}}$ reaches $0.99$. Matched-budget interventions show that improving feedback quality raises success from $0.27$ to $0.90$ while raw cost and tool calls are fixed. On mixed real traces, NRS-EFC/$D_{\mathrm{task}}$ reaches $R^2=0.92$ while raw compute has near-zero or negative fit, and it remains the best predictor in a prospective holdout ($R^2=0.85$). These results suggest that harness scaling is governed less by how much computation is spent than by how efficiently raw budget is converted into durable, task-sufficient feedback.

</details>


### [75] [Domino: Decoupling Causal Modeling from Autoregressive Drafting in Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29707)
*Jianuo Huang,Yaojie Zhang,Qituan Zhang,Hao Lin,Hanlin Xu,Linfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Domino是一种推测解码框架，通过将因果依赖建模与昂贵的自回归草稿执行解耦，先使用并行草稿骨干生成初步分布，再用轻量级Domino head进行因果修正，辅以基锚训练课程，在Qwen3模型上实现了高达5.49倍的端到端加速和5.8倍的吞吐量加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码方法在草稿质量与生成开销之间存在权衡：自回归草稿器能建模因果依赖但存在顺序开销，并行草稿器虽降低开销却削弱了块内依赖建模能力。Domino旨在打破这一权衡。

Method: 首先使用并行草稿骨干为整个块生成初步草稿分布，然后通过轻量级Domino head利用前缀相关的因果信息进行精炼；同时引入基锚训练课程，先强化并行骨干，再逐步将优化转向因果校正后的最终分布。

Result: 在Qwen3模型上，Domino在Transformers后端实现了最高5.49倍的端到端加速，在SGLang服务架构下实现了最高5.8倍的吞吐量加速。

Conclusion: Domino通过解耦因果依赖建模与自回归执行，有效克服了推测解码中草稿质量与开销之间的固有矛盾，实现了显著的加速效果。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by drafting multiple tokens and verifying them in parallel with the target model. However, its practical speedup is constrained by the trade-off between draft quality and drafting cost: autoregressive drafters model causal dependencies among draft tokens but incur sequential overhead, while parallel drafters reduce drafting cost but weaken intra-block dependency modeling. In this paper, we propose Domino, a speculative decoding framework that decouples causal dependency modeling from expensive autoregressive draft execution. Domino first uses a parallel draft backbone to produce preliminary draft distributions for the entire block, and then applies a lightweight Domino head to refine them with prefix-dependent causal information. To stabilize teacher-forced causal encoding, we further introduce a base-anchored training curriculum that first strengthens the parallel backbone and then gradually shifts optimization toward the causally corrected final distribution. Experiments on Qwen3 models show that Domino achieves up to \(5.49\times\) end-to-end speedup under the Transformers backend and up to \(5.8\times\) throughput speedup under SGLang serving.

</details>


### [76] [Understanding Safety-Sensitive Expert Behavior in Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29708)
*Zhibo Zhang,Yuxi Li,Zhen Ouyang,Ling Shi,Kailong Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现MoE大语言模型的路由模式主要由主题驱动，而非安全行为驱动；安全行为可以在几乎不改变路由路径的情况下被改变。基于此，提出RASET框架，通过对比路由敏感性标准识别安全关键专家，仅对选中的专家进行参数高效微调，揭示了MoE安全对齐的独特风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对MoE LLM中安全对齐如何与路由专家专业化相互作用的理解不足。直觉认为安全行为可能通过将有害请求路由到专门的拒绝专家来控制，但缺乏实证验证。

Method: 提出RASET红队框架，包含两个核心步骤：(1) 通过对比路由敏感性标准识别安全关键专家；(2) 仅对选中的专家进行参数高效微调，同时保持模型固有的路由行为不变。

Result: 实验证明路由模式主要由主题驱动，安全行为可以在几乎不改变模型内在路由路径的情况下被改变。RASET能够有效定位安全关键专家并进行微调，相比路由干预方法语义扰动更小。

Conclusion: MoE LLM存在独特的安全风险，安全对齐机制需要考虑到专家专业化特性，而非仅依赖路由控制。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) LLMs rely on sparse, router-driven expert activation, yet how safety alignment interacts with routed expert specialization remains underexplored. A common intuition is that safety behavior may be controlled by routing harmful requests to distinct refusal-oriented experts. In this work, we provide empirical evidence for a different picture: routing patterns in aligned MoE LLMs are largely topic-driven, while safety behavior can be altered with little change to the model's intrinsic routing path.
  Motivated by this observation, we present **RASET** (**R**outer-**A**gnostic **S**afety-critical **E**xpert **T**uning), a red-teaming framework that probes safety enforcement that is localized in a small subset of experts while preserving the model's intrinsic routing behavior. **RASET** identifies safety-critical experts via a contrastive routing-sensitivity criterion and applies parameter-efficient tuning only to the selected experts, minimizing semantic disruption relative to router-steering interventions. These results reveal a distinct MoE safety risk, highlighting the need for expert-aware alignment mechanisms.

</details>


### [77] [Personalized Turn-Level User Conversation Satisfaction Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29711)
*Zhefan Wang,Zhiqiang Guo,Weizhi Ma,Min Zhang,Quanjia Yan,Hengliang Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合个性化记忆与事后评分校准的对话满意度评估器，用于判断特定轮次下用户对AI助手回复的满意度，并构建了PersTurnBench基准，通过回放机制实现对生成模型的个性化满意度评估，无需为每个模型收集新的人工标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动评估方法大多衡量通用回复质量，难以判断特定轮次下用户是否满意，而用户满意度高度个性化，相同回复对不同用户可能截然不同。

Method: 构建结合紧凑用户记忆与目标轮次上下文的满意度评估器，生成满意度分数和面向不满意的理由；采用事后评分校准提升序数一致性和不满意轮次检测能力；构建PersTurnBench基准，利用验证后的评估器通过回放机制评估生成模型。

Result: 个性化记忆与事后评分校准在序数一致性和不满意轮次检测上优于监督、检索和通用LLM-as-a-judge基线。

Conclusion: 所提出的评估器和基准能够让研究人员在不收集每个模型的新用户反馈的情况下，比较候选生成模型在个性化满意度上的表现。

Abstract: User satisfaction with AI assistants is highly personalized: the same response may satisfy one user but disappoint another depending on what each user expects and what they have asked for before. Existing automatic evaluation methods mostly measure generic response quality, making it difficult to judge whether a response satisfies a user at a specific turn. We study this problem as personalized turn-level user conversation satisfaction evaluation. We build a conversation satisfaction evaluator that combines compact user memories with target-turn context to produce satisfaction scores and dissatisfaction-oriented rationales. Meta-evaluation against human satisfaction annotations shows that personalized memory and post-hoc score calibration improve ordinal agreement and dissatisfied-turn detection over supervised, retrieval-based, and generic LLM-as-a-judge baselines. We further introduce PersTurnBench, a personalized turn-level user conversation satisfaction benchmark that uses the verified evaluator to assess generation models via replay. By holding the replay state fixed, PersTurnBench enables controlled comparison of generic generation models and memory-augmented personalized systems without new human labels for every candidate model. The evaluator and benchmark let researchers compare candidate generation models on personalized satisfaction without collecting new user feedback for every model.

</details>


### [78] [Teaching Language Models to Check Grounded Claim Factuality with Human Test-Taking Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29712)
*Yuxuan Ye,Raul Santos-Rodriguez,Edwin Simpson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将事实性检查建模为真假阅读理解任务，通过提示LLM使用考试策略进行高效推理，相比无约束推理节省80%以上token消耗，在两个基准上达到竞争性能，并利用监督微调和自我修正机制训练小语言模型替代LLM，在低推理成本下达到强基线水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于蕴含分类器的事实性检查指标需要针对数据集调整阈值，而基于LLM的直接提示方法未能充分利用其推理能力，导致效率低下。

Method: 将事实性检查转化为真假阅读理解任务，提示LLM使用明确的考试策略进行推理；同时通过监督微调和自我修正机制训练小语言模型来替代大语言模型，降低推理成本。

Result: 相比无约束推理，token使用量减少80%以上；在两个事实性基准上取得有竞争力的性能，其中一个达到新最优；训练的小语言模型在低推理成本下与强基线持平，并能生成支撑性理由。

Conclusion: 将事实性检查建模为阅读理解任务并辅以考试策略提示，可显著提升LLM推理效率；通过SFT和自修正训练的SLM能以低代价达到可比性能，兼具可解释性。

Abstract: Grounded claim factuality checking is important for large language model (LLM) applications such as retrieval-augmented generation, as it helps users assess the correctness of generated outputs. Existing metrics using entailment classifiers require dataset-specific threshold tuning, while LLM-based approaches often use direct prompting, which underutilises the reasoning capabilities of LLMs. We address this by formulating grounded claim factuality checking as a true/false reading comprehension task and prompting LLMs with explicit test-taking strategies for efficient reasoning. Our method reduces token usage by over 80% compared to unguided open-ended reasoning, and achieves competitive performance to more expensive alternatives across two factuality benchmarks, setting a new state of the art on one. To further reduce inference cost, we train small language models (SLMs) to replace LLMs in the checking pipeline. Using supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and a self-revision mechanism, the SLMs learn to improve their factuality judgements. Experimental results show that the resulting SLMs perform on par with strong baselines, combining low inference costs with generating supporting rationales to support interpretability. Code and datasets will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [79] [Leveraging Routing Dynamics in Mixture-of-Experts Models for Efficient Language Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29714)
*Aditi Khandelwal,Marius Mosbach,Verna Dankers,Siva Reddy,Golnoosh Farnadi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了以英语为中心的MoE模型在多语言持续预训练中的路由动态，发现语言无关的路由出现在早期和中间层，而语言专业化主要出现在最后层。基于此，提出了一种仅更新最后MoE层中语言特定和共享专家的参数高效适配策略，在更新不到2%参数的情况下取得了与微调完整层相当的竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型被广泛用于扩展语言模型，但其在多语言环境下的专家路由行为和适应性尚未得到充分探索。作者旨在理解多语言持续预训练中MoE模型的语言路由动态，并据此提出高效的适配策略。

Method: 首先分析英语中心MoE模型在多语言语料上持续预训练时的路由动态，重点关注专家使用在不同语言间的变化。研究发现早期和中间层呈现扩散的语言无关路由，语言专业化主要在最后层出现，且词元级词汇重叠影响路由。基于发现，提出一种参数高效适配方法，仅更新最后MoE层中的语言特定专家和共享专家。

Result: 在MultiBLiMP和Belebele基准上的实验表明，该方法在更新不到2%参数的情况下，达到了与微调完整最后层相当的竞争性能，实现了强性能-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了MoE模型在多语言持续预训练中语言专业化的出现位置和方式，为低资源多语言适配提供了实用见解。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models are widely used to scale language models, yet their expert routing behavior and adaptation in a multilingual setting remain underexplored. In this work, we study multilingual routing dynamics during continual pre-training of an English-centric MoE model on a multilingual corpus, analyzing how expert usage varies across languages. We find that continual multilingual pre-training leads to diffused, language-agnostic routing in early and middle layers, with language specialization primarily emerging in the final layers. We also show that token-level vocabulary overlap between languages plays an important role in how languages are routed. Motivated by these findings, we propose a parameter-efficient adaptation strategy that updates language-specific and shared experts in the final MoE layers. Experiments on MultiBLiMP and Belebele show that our method achieves a strong performance-efficiency trade-off, attaining competitive performance relative to fine-tuning complete final layers, while updating less than 2% of the parameters. Overall, our findings provide insights into where and how language specialization emerges in MoEs during continual pre-training and provide practical insights for low-resource multilingual adaptation. Our code is available at https://github.com/aditi184/moe-routing-adaptation.

</details>


### [80] [User-Aware Active Knowledge Acquisition for Emotional Support Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29715)
*Mufan Xu,Kehai Chen,Jiahao Hu,Xinchao Xu,Muyun Yang,Tiejun Zhao,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需梯度的主动对话学习框架UKA，通过理论心智不确定性估计机制，在多轮对话中主动获取用户对齐的知识，提升情感支持对话系统的用户对齐能力和对话质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感支持对话方法难以高效获取和泛化与用户需求相关的对话知识，因为用户需求的信号往往微弱、间接，且只能通过多轮交互来消歧。

Method: 提出UKA框架，包含：(1) Theory-of-Mind不确定性估计机制，用于优先选择能引发更多信息性用户反馈的回应；(2) 将主动学习融入知识获取和回应选择两个阶段；(3) 无梯度主动对话学习框架。

Result: 在多个对话基准和模型架构上，UKA在对话质量和用户对齐方面持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: UKA框架能高效探索用户对齐的对话知识，在训练时学习、测试时保持鲁棒性，有效解决了情感支持对话中用户需求信号微弱、需多轮交互消歧的挑战。

Abstract: Emotional support plays an important role in dialogue systems, and its success depends on adapting to a user's evolving and implicit needs across multi-turn interactions while leveraging the strong reasoning capacity of large language models. However, since signals about user needs are often weak, indirect, and can only be disambiguated through multi-turn interaction, existing emotional support methods often struggle to acquire and generalize relevant conversational knowledge efficiently. To bridge this gap, we introduce User-Aware Active Knowledge Acquisition (UKA), a gradient-free active dialogue learning framework that explicitly represents uncertainty about user needs and incorporates active learning into both knowledge acquisition and response selection.We propose a Theory-of-Mind uncertainty estimation mechanism that allows the model to prioritize responses, thereby eliciting more informative user feedback. UKA is capable of efficiently exploring user-aligned conversational knowledge during training while maintaining robustness at test time. Experiments across multiple dialogue benchmarks and model architectures demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms strong baselines in dialogue quality and user alignment.

</details>


### [81] [HTAM: Hierarchical Transition-Attended Memory for Operator Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29734)
*Yining Zhang,Mingyang Yi,Chen Wang,Xuwen Xiang,Tianhe Jia,Zedong Dan,Chengqing Zong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HTAM提出了一种粗到细的分层框架（Hierarchical Transition-Attended Memory），通过构建两级层次转换图来组织优化经验，解决了LLM生成GPU算子时粒度不匹配的问题，在KernelBench上显著提升了正确率、快速解率和加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM-based GPU算子优化方法面临粒度不匹配问题：粗粒度提示可重用但难以执行，细粒度记忆虽可操作但扩大搜索空间且模糊优化瓶颈。因此需要以合适的粒度组织优化经验。

Method: 提出HTAM框架，构建两级层次转换图（HTG）组织粗粒度的全局方向、细粒度的局部策略以及优化步骤间的转换经验。在每个演化步骤中，基于当前状态和优化历史选择全局方向，检索对应的局部策略记忆，并用于引导具体的CUDA代码生成。

Result: 在完整KernelBench套件上，HTAM相比基于LLM的基线方法一致提升了正确率、快速解率和加速比；后端和Robust-KBench实验表明结构化记忆具有可迁移的收益。

Conclusion: HTAM通过分层的经验组织方式有效解决了粒度不匹配问题，证明了结构化记忆在LLM驱动的高性能GPU算子优化中的有效性。

Abstract: High-performance GPU kernels are essential for efficient LLM deployment, yet optimizing them remains expertise-intensive. Recent LLM-based code generation makes automatic GPU operator generation promising, but operator optimization remains a hardware-aware search problem. Existing LLM-based methods face a granularity mismatch: coarse hints are reusable but hard to execute, whereas detailed memories are actionable but enlarge the search space and obscure optimization bottlenecks. The key challenge is therefore to organize optimization experience at an appropriate granularity. To address this issue, this paper proposes HTAM (Hierarchical Transition-Attended Memory), a coarse-to-fine framework for LLM-based operator optimization. HTAM builds a two-level Hierarchical Transition Graph (HTG) to organize coarse global directions, detailed local strategies, and transition experience between optimization steps. During each evolution step, HTAM selects a global direction from the current state and recent optimization history, retrieves the corresponding local strategy memory, and uses it to guide concrete CUDA code generation. Experiments on the full KernelBench suite demonstrate that HTAM consistently improves correctness, fast-solution rate, and speedup over LLM-based baselines, while backend and Robust-KBench studies indicate transferable benefits from structured memory.

</details>


### [82] [Multi-Legal-Bench: Evaluating LLMs on Legal Reasoning Across Jurisdictions, Languages, and Legal Traditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29738)
*Volodymyr Ovcharov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Multi-Legal-Bench 是首个跨司法管辖区的法律 NLP 基准，涵盖 6 个国家、4 个语系和 1.34 亿份法院判决，通过 5 项任务评估 11 个模型，发现少样本效果具有任务依赖性、无单一模型跨语言主导、跨语言迁移遵循标签对齐而非语言亲缘性、分词器效率不显著预测跨语言准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律 NLP 基准大多评估单一语言或聚合不同司法管辖区的基本不同任务，导致跨语言比较无法进行。

Method: 构建 Multi-Legal-Bench 基准，包含 5 项任务（法院类型分类、判决形式分类、案件结果预测、法律规范抽取、案由类别预测）在 6 个国家（乌克兰、法国、荷兰、波兰、捷克、立陶宛）上形成稀疏的 5×6 任务-司法管辖区矩阵（30 个单元格填充 20 个）。评估 7 个前沿 LLM（零样本和 3 样本）及 4 个小/中型模型进行规模分析。

Result: (1) 乌克兰发现的少样本效果任务依赖性在所有管辖区复制；(2) 无单一模型跨语言主导，排名随任务和管辖区变化；(3) 跨语言少样本迁移不遵循语言亲缘性（UA→FR Romance 语系 -2.1pp 优于 UA→PL Slavic 语系 -13.7pp），标签集对齐比语言家族更能预测迁移质量；(4) 分词器 fertility 虽达 2.3 倍差异，但不显著预测跨语言准确性（r=-0.27, p=0.14），模型架构和预训练数据占主导。

Conclusion: 发布了所有数据、提示和模型预测，为跨司法管辖区法律 NLP 研究提供了标准化评估平台。

Abstract: Legal NLP benchmarks overwhelmingly evaluate a single language or aggregate tasks that differ fundamentally across jurisdictions, making cross-lingual comparison impossible. We introduce Multi-Legal-Bench, the first cross-jurisdictional legal benchmark that evaluates identical tasks across six countries (Ukraine, France, Netherlands, Poland, Czech Republic, Lithuania), four language families, and 134 million court decisions. The benchmark defines five tasks court-type classification, judgment form classification, case-outcome prediction, legal norm extraction, and cause category prediction mapped to structured metadata from national court registries, forming a deliberately sparse 5x6 task-jurisdiction matrix (20 of 30 cells filled). We evaluate 7 frontier LLMs under zero-shot and 3-shot prompting via AWS Bedrock, with 4 additional small/medium models (3-12B) for scaling analysis. Our results reveal that: (1) task-dependent few-shot effects discovered in Ukrainian replicate across all jurisdictions; (2) no single model dominates any language rankings shift with both task and jurisdiction; (3) cross-lingual few-shot transfer does not follow language proximity: UA->FR (Romance, -2.1 pp) transfers better than UA->PL (Slavic, -13.7 pp), with label-set alignment predicting transfer quality better than language family; and (4) tokenizer fertility, despite a 2.3x spread, does not significantly predict cross-lingual accuracy (r=-0.27, p=0.14), suggesting that model architecture and pretraining data dominate tokenizer efficiency. We release all data, prompts, and model predictions.

</details>


### [83] [AfriScience-MT: Towards Decolonizing Science in Africa through Text Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29741)
*Idris Abdulmumin,Tajuddeen Gwadabe,Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad,David Ifeoluwa Adelani,Nomonde Khalo,Ibrahim Said Ahmad,Abiodun Modupe,Anina Mumm,Sibusiso Biyela,Michelle Rabie,Johanna Havemann,Marek Rei,Jade Abbott,Vukosi Marivate*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了AfriScience-MT平行语料库，涵盖6种非洲语言和11个科学领域，通过专业翻译和术语创新解决了非洲语言科学术语缺失问题，并系统评估了多种机器翻译模型。闭源模型表现最佳，开源模型中微调NLLB-1.3B和TranslateGemma-12B分别达到句子级和文档级最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 殖民语言在非洲教育和科学传播中占主导地位，限制了数亿非洲语言使用者获取和产生科学知识，核心障碍是缺乏已建立的非洲语言科学术语体系。

Method: 构建AfriScience-MT平行语料库，涵盖阿姆哈拉语、豪萨语、卢干达语、北索托语、约鲁巴语和祖鲁语6种非洲语言及11个科学领域；由专业翻译与科学传播专家合作翻译科学论文的通俗摘要，并创造新术语。在零样本、少样本和微调设置下，对机器翻译系统和大型语言模型进行基准测试。

Result: 闭源模型在所有设置中优于开源模型：GPT-5.4和Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite在句子级COMET得分分别为68.3和68.0，文档级均为48.3。开源模型中，微调NLLB-1.3B在句子级达67.3，1-shot上下文学习中TranslateGemma-12B在文档级达44.0。

Conclusion: AfriScience-MT语料库的发布将为非洲语言的机器翻译基准测试和文档级科学翻译提供重要资源，有助于弥合非洲语言在科学交流中的鸿沟。

Abstract: The dominance of colonial languages in African education and scientific communication limits how hundreds of millions of speakers of African languages access and produce scientific knowledge. A core obstacle is the lack of established scientific terminology in these languages. We introduce AfriScience-MT, a parallel corpus covering six African languages (Amharic, Hausa, Luganda, Northern Sotho, Yorùbá, and isiZulu) across 11 scientific domains. Professional translators, working with expert science communicators, translated plain-language summaries of scientific papers into each target language and created new terms where none existed. We benchmark machine translation systems and large language models in zero-shot, few-shot, and fine-tuned settings. Our results show that closed-source models outperform all open-source models at both the sentence and document levels: GPT-5.4 and Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite lead with average sentence-level COMET scores of 68.3 and 68.0, respectively, and tie at an average document-level COMET of 48.3. Among open systems, fine-tuned NLLB-1.3B reaches 67.3 at the sentence level, and TranslateGemma-12B reaches 44.0 at the document level with 1-shot in-context learning. We release AfriScience-MT to support benchmarking and document-level scientific MT for African languages.

</details>


### [84] [DySem: Uncovering Dynamic Semantic Components via Multilingual Consensus for Calculating Semantic Textual Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29751)
*Kaijie Zheng,Weiqin Wang,Yile Wang,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DySem提出了一种无需训练的新框架，通过多语言共识挖掘LLM中与语义更相关的内部组件，并构建文本依赖的动态联合语义集合来计算语义相似度，解决了传统依赖最后一层隐藏状态存在的语义不纯和维度冗余噪音问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于LLM的语义文本相似度计算方法通常依赖最后一层隐藏状态，但该方法存在两个问题：(1)最后一层编码的是一般知识而非纯语义知识；(2)隐藏层维度过大，包含冗余和噪音，不利于语义相似度的准确计算。

Method: 提出DySem框架：通过多语言共识机制发现LLM中与语义更相关的内部组件，并构造文本依赖的联合语义集合，在该共享维度子集上计算相似度，实现从静态表示空间到动态、样本特定语义维度的转变，且无需训练。

Result: 在多种LLM上的广泛实验表明，DySem在保持更低维度的相似度计算的同时，一致性地优于近期基线方法。

Conclusion: DySem通过探索LLM内部更语义相关的组件并采用动态维度选择策略，有效提升了语义文本相似度计算的性能，为无需额外训练的语义表示方法提供了新思路。

Abstract: Calculating semantic textual similarity is a foundational task in natural language processing. Current large language models (LLMs) based methods typically rely on extracting last-layer hidden states with fixed dimensions to compute similarity for every text pairs. We argue that this paradigm is suffer from two limitations: (i) The last hidden layer encodes more general knowledge rather than just semantic knowledge, making it suboptimal for semantic similarity computation; (ii) The hidden layer dimensions of LLMs are generally very large, which introduces some redundancy and noise for representing semantics. In this work, we propose DySem, a novel training-free framework that investigates more semantic-related internal components of LLMs via multilingual consensus, and shifts away from static representation spaces in favor of dynamic, sample-specific semantic dimensions by constructing text-dependent joint semantic set and computes similarity over this shared dimensional subset. Extensive experiments across various LLMs show that our method consistently outperforms recent baselines while maintaining lower dimensions for similarity calculation. The code is released at https://github.com/szu-tera/DySem.

</details>


### [85] [ActTraitBench: Quantifying the Knowledge-Decision Gap in Large Language Models via Human-Grounded Behavioral Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29791)
*Yutong Yang,Chenxi Miao,Weikang Li,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ActTraitBench框架，通过人类实证数据建立心理测量维度与行为范式的映射，并用量位映射校准LLM评分分布，揭示了大语言模型在自我报告与行为决策之间存在知识-决策差距，并引入CoCA方法缓解该差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准在评估LLM人格一致性时面临构念效度有限、多维度纠缠以及基于LLM评估的分布偏差等问题，难以准确测量LLM在自我报告与行为决策间的知识-决策差距。

Method: 提出ActTraitBench框架：基于人类实证数据建立心理测量维度与行为范式的一一映射；采用分布校准（Quantile Mapping）方法对齐LLM评委的评分分布与人类规范分布。同时引入Chain of Cognitive Alignment (CoCA)作为推理时干预方法。

Result: 在14个主流LLM上的实验揭示了普遍的知识-决策不对称现象：更大、更强的模型尽管自我报告高度一致，但行为分歧更为显著。CoCA方法能改善推理能力强的前沿模型的对齐，但在较小架构中暴露了能力局限性。

Conclusion: LLM存在自我报告与行为决策间的系统性差距，ActTraitBench能有效评估该差距，CoCA可作为缓解手段，但效果受限于模型本身的能力水平。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) can convincingly simulate personas in explicit self-reports, they often deviate in implicit behavioral decisions, revealing a substantial Knowledge-Decision Gap ($G_{\text{KD}}$). Existing benchmarks struggle to measure this asymmetry due to limited construct validity, multi-dimensional entanglement, and distributional biases in LLM-based evaluation. To address these issues, we propose ActTraitBench, a human-grounded evaluation framework for measuring personality consistency in LLMs. Grounded in empirical human data, ActTraitBench establishes one-to-one mappings between psychometric facets and behavioral paradigms, and applies a Distributional Calibration via Quantile Mapping procedure to align LLM-judge score distributions with human norms. Experiments on 14 mainstream LLMs reveal a pervasive knowledge-decision asymmetry, where larger and more capable models often exhibit stronger behavioral divergence despite highly consistent self-reports. To mitigate this gap, we further introduce the Chain of Cognitive Alignment (CoCA), a plug-and-play inference-time intervention that improves alignment in reasoning-capable frontier models while exposing clear capability limitations in smaller architectures.

</details>


### [86] [Metric-Dependent Annotation Saturation for Learning from Label Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29797)
*Guneet Kohli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了NLI任务中标注者 disagreement 的信号价值，发现不同评估指标对标注者数量需求存在差异：熵相关性（识别争议项）需20-50人收敛，而KL散度（分布匹配）在10人时即饱和（87-95%改进）。软标签携带的项目特异性信号无法被标签平滑复制（平滑聚类r~0.45-0.49，软标签r=0.643, p&lt;0.001），该优势在多种架构和跨领域评估中保持稳健。建议标注预算应根据目标评估指标确定而非统一设定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究标注者 disagreement 中蕴含的信号价值，以及不同评估指标下所需标注者数量的差异，优化标注预算分配策略。

Method: 基于ChaosNLI数据集（每项100个独立标注）对NLI模型进行标签分布子采样微调；对比软标签与标签平滑的效果；在DeBERTa、RoBERTa等架构及跨领域内容安全任务上验证。

Result: 熵相关性收敛需20-50标注者，KL散度在10人时饱和（87-95%改进）；软标签熵相关性r=0.643显著优于平滑的r~0.45-0.49（p&lt;0.001）；该优势在多种架构和跨领域任务中稳健。

Conclusion: 标注预算应依据目标评估指标动态设定，而非统一标准；软标签能捕获标注者 disagreement 中的项目特异性信号，这是标签平滑无法替代的。

Abstract: When annotators disagree on a label, the disagreement itself carries signal -- and the number of annotators needed to capture it depends on the evaluation metric. We fine-tune NLI models on label distributions subsampled from ChaosNLI, a dataset providing 100 independent annotator judgments per item, and identify metric-dependent saturation. In our 3-class NLI setting, entropy correlation -- whether the model identifies which items elicit disagreement -- requires N ~ 20-50 annotators to converge, while distributional match (KL divergence) saturates by N ~ 10 (87-95% of improvement across five model seeds). This finding rests on a prior observation: soft labels carry item-specific signal that label smoothing cannot replicate. Across five smoothing intensities, entropy correlation clusters at r ~ 0.45-0.49, while soft labels reach r = 0.643 (p < 0.001); per-item analysis traces this gap to smoothing's inability to distinguish ambiguous items from clear ones. The soft-label advantage replicates across two architectures (DeBERTa, RoBERTa), a non-NLI-pretrained baseline, and an exploratory cross-domain evaluation on content safety. These results suggest that annotation budgets should be informed by the target evaluation metric rather than set uniformly.

</details>


### [87] [Nine Judges, Two Effective Votes: Correlated Errors Undermine LLM Evaluation Panels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29800)
*Guneet Kohli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM评审团看似由多个模型构成，但实际有效信息仅相当于约2个独立投票者。9个前沿模型组成的评审团因模型间高度相关（犯相同错误），其投票独立性损失约3/4，准确性比理想独立投票低8-22个百分点，且最佳单个模型的表现已不逊于整个评审团。增加法官数量或改进聚合算法均无法解决这一瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化LLM评审团的实际信息价值，揭示其与理想独立投票之间的差距，并探究这一差距的根本原因。

Method: 采用Kish有效样本量（n_eff）和Condorcet零模型作为量化框架，在三个自然语言推理数据集（每项含100个人工标注）上测试了来自7个模型家族的9个前沿LLM，同时验证了提示变体、温度、思维链推理及成对偏好任务（RewardBench）等条件下的鲁棒性。

Result: 9个评审模型仅提供约2个独立投票的有效信息；名义独立性损失约3/4源于模型在相同项目上犯相同错误；实际准确性比独立投票理想值低8-22个百分点；最佳单模型在所有条件下均匹配或超越整个评审团；已有聚合方法最多仅能弥合11%的差距，即使使用正确答案也无法解决。

Conclusion: LLM评审团的瓶颈在于评委之间的高度相关性，而非聚合算法。扩大评审团规模无法替代真正的独立评估。要实现更可靠的LLM评估，需要从根本上提升模型之间的独立性。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge panels aggregate votes from multiple models, with the expectation that diverse models yield more reliable evaluations. We develop a framework to measure the true informational value of such panels and quantify how far their reliability falls short of the independent-voting ideal. Testing a panel of 9 frontier LLMs from 7 model families on three natural language inference datasets (each with 100 human annotations per item), we find that the 9 judges effectively provide only about 2 independent votes' worth of information. Roughly three-quarters of the panel's nominal independence is lost because the models make the same mistakes on the same items. The consequences are stark: the panel's actual accuracy falls 8-22 percentage points short of what independent voting would achieve, and the best single judge matches or outperforms the full panel across all conditions. Neither adding more judges nor using smarter aggregation algorithms helps -- established methods close at most 11% of this gap, even with access to the correct answers. We quantify these findings using the Kish effective sample size (n_eff) and a Condorcet null model, and show the deficit is robust across prompt variants, temperatures, chain-of-thought reasoning, and a pairwise preference task (RewardBench). The bottleneck is correlated judges, not the aggregation algorithm, implying that scaling up panels cannot substitute for genuinely independent evaluation.

</details>


### [88] [Data filtering methods for training language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29807)
*Egor Shevchenko,Elena Bruches*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了Confident Learning和Dataset Cartography两种自动标签错误检测方法在三个俄语文本分类数据集上的效果，发现方法有效性高度依赖数据集特性：在大规模低噪声数据集上过滤无效，在小规模高噪声数据集上Confident Learning显著提升F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据质量对机器学习模型至关重要，标签错误普遍存在且损害模型泛化能力。现有基准数据集中存在标签噪声，需要有效的自动检测方法来识别和过滤这些错误标签。

Method: 在三个俄语文本分类语料库（ru_emotion_e-culture 49,123条、RuCoLA 8,524条、TERRa 2,337条）上，使用预训练rubert-base-cased模型微调后，对比Confident Learning和Dataset Cartography两种标签错误检测方法，并通过随机删除等量样本进行对照实验。

Result: 两种方法的效果强烈依赖数据集特征：大规模低噪声数据集上过滤不提升性能；小规模高噪声数据集上Confident Learning实现显著的F1-macro提升；Dataset Cartography更保守，删除样本更少；两种方法的目标移除均优于随机移除。

Conclusion: 标签错误检测的有效性与数据集规模、噪声水平密切相关，两种方法各有适用场景：Confident Learning适合小规模高噪声数据，Dataset Cartography更保守稳健，两种方法都优于随机基线，验证了自动标签错误检测的实用价值。

Abstract: Data quality is a critical factor in the effectiveness of machine learning models. Label errors, present even in widely used benchmarks, introduce noise into training data and reduce model generalization. In this work, we conduct a comparative analysis of two automatic label error detection methods - Confident Learning and Dataset Cartography - on three Russian text classification corpora of varying size, number of classes, and domain: ru_emotion_e-culture (49,123 examples, emotion classification), RuCoLA (8,524 examples, linguistic acceptability), and TERRa (2,337 examples, textual entailment recognition). We use the pre-trained rubert-base-cased model fine-tuned on each corpus. To verify the meaningfulness of filtering, we conduct control experiments with random removal of an equivalent number of examples. Results show that the effectiveness of both methods depends strongly on dataset characteristics: on large corpora with low noise levels, filtering does not improve performance, while on small datasets with high noise, Confident Learning achieves a significant F1-macro improvement. Dataset Cartography demonstrates more conservative behavior, removing fewer examples. Across all corpora, targeted removal by both methods outperforms random removal, confirming the meaningfulness of the approaches.

</details>


### [89] [Towards Localized and Disentangled Knowledge Editing for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29826)
*Leijiang Gu,Zhen Zeng,Feng Li,Xinjian Gao,Zenglin Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LDKE通过定位事实特定层和解耦相关/无关输入，解决了多模态知识编辑中的因果错位与特征纠缠问题，实现精准且泛化的编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MKE方法虽能修改目标事实对，但无法泛化到逻辑相关查询，且会意外改变无关但视觉/语义关联的信息。作者识别出两种失败模式：因果错位（编辑局限于特定样本）和特征纠缠（对耦合的无关信息造成意外修改）。

Method: 提出LDKE框架，包含两个核心模块：1) 快速定位模块（Fast Localization），识别并高效更新关键层；2) 解耦分类器（Disentanglement Classifier），路由输入以保留无关知识。

Result: 在多种基准和MLLM上的大量实验表明，LDKE在将编辑传播到相关上下文方面表现优越，同时保持了高局部性。

Conclusion: LDKE通过解耦策略实现了多模态知识编辑的精确性和泛化能力，有效克服了现有方法的局限性。

Abstract: Existing methods in Multimodal Knowledge Editing (MKE) have advanced the ability to correct outdated or inaccurate knowledge in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). However, they exhibit a critical limitation: while effectively modifying target factual pairs, they fail to generalize edits to logically related queries and often cause unintended alterations to unrelated but visually or semantically linked information. We identify and formalize two underlying failure modes causing this issue: Causal Misalignment, which confines edits to the specific sample, and Feature Entanglement, which causes unintended alterations to coupled but irrelevant information. To address these issues, we propose Localized and Disentangled Knowledge Editing (LDKE), a new framework that achieves precise and generalized editing by localizing fact-specific model layers and disentangling target-relevant inputs from irrelevant ones. Our approach introduces a Fast Localization module to identify and update critical layers efficiently, along with a Disentanglement Classifier that routes inputs appropriately to preserve unrelated knowledge. Extensive experiments across various benchmarks and MLLMs demonstrate that LDKE achieves superior performance in propagating edits to related contexts while maintaining high locality.

</details>


### [90] [EvoRubric: Self-Evolving Rubric-Driven RL for Open-Ended Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29847)
*Xin Guan,Xiaomeng Hu,Shen Huang,Zhenyi Wang,Bo Zhang,Zijian Li,Pengjun Xie,Bo Liu,Jiuxin Cao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EvoRubric提出了一种单策略协同进化RL框架，通过将响应生成与评分标准生成统一在同一参数化策略下，动态交替Reasoner和Rubric Generator角色，并引入多层级验证管线（元验证器、零方差剪枝、留一法同行共识）防止奖励黑客攻击，在医疗、写作和科学领域显著优于传统静态和外部LLM驱动的对齐方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于评分标准的RL方法要么依赖静态人工标注标准导致策略滞后，要么依赖昂贵的外部专有模型进行动态更新，缺乏一种不依赖静态标准和外部评分标准生成器的对齐方法。

Method: 提出单策略协同进化RL框架EvoRubric，将响应生成和评分标准生成统一在同一参数化策略下，动态交替Reasoner与Rubric Generator角色；引入多层级验证管线（元验证器、零方差剪枝、留一法同行共识）防止奖励黑客攻击；将验证后的标准动态归档到记忆池，产生密集多目标奖励以协同优化两个角色。

Result: 在医疗、写作和科学领域的广泛实验中，EvoRubric持续优于传统静态方法和外部LLM驱动的对齐方法；与人类专家先验兼容，利用专家标注标准初始化后可发现新的判别性维度，性能超越单纯依赖静态专家标注的方法。

Conclusion: EvoRubric通过单策略协同进化有效解决了开放生成场景中对齐的挑战，消除了对静态标准和外部评分标准生成器的依赖，同时通过多层级验证保证了生成信号的可信度，为开放域LLM对齐提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has significantly advanced Large Language Models (LLMs) in verifiable domains, but aligning models for open-ended generation remains profoundly challenging due to the lack of definitive rewards. Current rubric-based RL methods mitigate this by employing explicit criteria; however, they rely heavily on static, human-annotated rubrics that inevitably cause policy lag, or expensive external proprietary models for dynamic updates. In this paper, we propose EvoRubric, a novel single-policy co-evolutionary RL framework that eliminates the reliance on static criteria and on external rubric generators. By unifying response generation and rubric generation under a single parameterized policy, EvoRubric dynamically alternates between a Reasoner and a Rubric Generator. To prevent reward hacking and ensure the reliability of generated signals, we introduce a multi-level verification pipeline featuring a meta-verifier, zero-variance pruning, and a Leave-One-Out peer consensus mechanism. Validated criteria are dynamically archived into a memory pool, yielding dense, multi-objective rewards to continuously co-optimize both roles. Extensive experiments across Medical, Writing, and Science domains demonstrate that EvoRubric consistently outperforms traditional static and external-LLM-driven alignment methods. Notably, our framework is compatible with human-expert priors. When initialized with expert-annotated rubrics, EvoRubric can further uncover novel, discriminative dimensions, achieving better performance than relying solely on static expert annotations.

</details>


### [91] [Towards Verifiable Multimodal Deep Research: A Multi-Agent Harness for Interleaved Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29861)
*Chenghao Zhang,Guanting Dong,Yufan Liu,Tong Zhao,Zhicheng Dou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Ptah多智能体框架，通过规划-研究-写作三阶段和视觉工作记忆机制，生成可验证的多模态深度研究报告，并引入PtahEval评估协议，在基准测试上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的深度研究在可验证的多模态报告生成方面面临挑战：开放式的综合缺乏确定性真实标准，且需要将文本论证与视觉证据交错结合。

Method: 提出Ptah多智能体框架，包含规划、研究、写作三阶段：规划阶段构建视觉感知计划，研究阶段收集基于主张的证据并维护源对齐的图像在视觉工作记忆中，写作阶段通过声明式多模态工具组合生成报告。引入验证智能体作为验收函数，确保事实基础、引用忠实性和跨模态一致性。同时提出PtahEval评估协议，增加图像级和呈现级评估。

Result: 在深度研究基准测试上，Ptah生成的报告比强基线更可靠、视觉信息更丰富、对用户更可用。

Conclusion: Ptah通过多智能体协同和视觉工作记忆机制，有效解决了多模态深度研究报告的可验证性和视觉-文本交错生成问题。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have advanced autonomous agents from deep search, which retrieves concise factual answers, to deep research, which synthesizes scattered evidence into long-form reports. However, verifiable multimodal deep research remains challenging due to open-ended synthesis without deterministic ground truth and the need to interleave textual arguments with visual evidence. We propose \textsc{Ptah}, a multi-agent harness for interleaved report generation. \textsc{Ptah} orchestrates the lifecycle from user query to rendered web report through planning, research, and writing stages, where specialized agents construct visual-aware plans, collect claim-grounded evidence, maintain source-aligned images in a \textit{Visual Working Memory}, and compose reports through declarative multimodal tool use. A verifier agent serves as the harness's acceptance function, enforcing factual grounding, citation fidelity, and cross-modal consistency throughout the workflow. We further introduce \textsc{Ptah}Eval, an evaluation protocol that augments existing benchmarks with image-level and presentation-level assessments. Experiments on deep research benchmarks show that \textsc{Ptah} produces more reliable, visually informative, and usable human-facing multimodal reports than strong baselines.

</details>


### [92] [CRITIC-R1: Learning Structured Critics for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29886)
*Wenhan Xiao,Ziwei Zhang,Chuanyue Yu,Xingcheng Fu,Qingyun Sun,Runhua Xu,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CRITIC-R1 提出了一种基于强化学习的结构化批评框架，将 RAG 输出的错误诊断分解为多个维度（裁决、定位、推理、修复），通过两种奖励函数（CJA 和 DQA）缓解过度干预问题，并利用 GRPO 和教师模型的过程监督进行训练，在五个 QA 基准上显著提升了答案质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG 方法仍存在幻觉和推理错误；外部批评者虽能改进 RAG 输出，但提供的反馈粗粒度、结构弱、干预过于激进，导致噪声大且不可靠，限制了纠正效果。

Method: 将批评形式化为显式的错误诊断问题，划分为裁决、错误定位、推理分析和修复生成四个维度；设计保守判断对齐（CJA）奖励校准高层面判决并缓解过度干预，以及诊断质量对齐（DQA）奖励通过门控机制提升细粒度诊断反馈；采用 GRPO 强化学习结合外部 LLM 教师模型的过程级监督训练批评模型。

Result: 在五个问答基准测试上，CRITIC-R1 持续优于强 RAG 基线，显著提升了答案质量。

Conclusion: 结构化、细粒度的批评框架配合强化学习能有效减少 RAG 幻觉与推理错误，解决过度干预问题，是提升 RAG 可靠性的有效路径。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves knowledge-intensive question answering by incorporating external evidence. However, existing RAG methods still suffer from hallucinations and subtle reasoning errors. Recent studies introduce external critics to refine RAG outputs, yet they often provide coarse-grained and weakly structured feedback, exhibit over-aggressive intervention, and lead to noisy and unreliable refinement, limiting their effectiveness for correction. To tackle these issues, we propose CRITIC-R1, a structured critic framework that formulates and learns RAG critique as an explicit error diagnosis problem using reinforcement learning (RL). Our framework categorizes common RAG errors into multiple diagnostic dimensions, including verdict, error location, reasoning analysis, and fix generation. To learn these capabilities, we design two reward functions: Conservative Judgement Alignment (CJA) first encourages calibrated high-level judgements while mitigating the over-aggressive phenomenon, whereas Diagnostic Quality Alignment (DQA) further improves fine-grained diagnostic feedback through gated rewards. We train the critic model using GRPO-based RL with process-level supervision collected from external LLM teacher models. Experiments across five QA benchmarks show that CRITIC-R1 consistently improves answer quality over strong RAG baselines.
  Our source code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/critic-r1-FCB0

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [93] [One Mask to Rule Them All: On Hidden Facts after Editing and How to Find Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28839)
*Ali Holmov,Paul Youssef,Nandi Schoots,Christin Seifert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现ROME和MEMIT等知识编辑方法虽然修改不同事实时权重变化不同，但均依赖一个共同的功能子空间（通过二进制掩码可逆转80%训练集和70%测试集编辑）。该机制通过抑制而非覆盖知识起作用，导致编辑无法传播到相关事实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识编辑方法（如ROME、MEMIT）主要通过输出行为评估，其内部机制尚不明确。本文旨在探究不同事实编辑是否共享共同的运作机制。

Method: 训练一个紧凑的二进制掩码来识别编辑权重中的关键子集，通过掩码逆转编辑效果；分析掩码对注意力模式的影响；通过注入掩码观察编辑成功率变化。

Result: 二进制掩码可逆转80%训练集和70%以上测试集的编辑效果；掩码通过消除后期层的过度注意力来逆转编辑；注入掩码使编辑成功率从98%骤降至38%。

Conclusion: 不同事实编辑共享共同的功能子空间，编辑机制是抑制而非覆盖原有知识，这解释了编辑无法传播到相关事实的原因；该共同子空间可用于检测和防御不当编辑。

Abstract: Knowledge editing methods such as ROME and MEMIT update factual associations in transformer models by modifying MLP weights. While evaluated mainly by output behavior, their internal mechanism remains underexplored. We investigate whether edits rely on a common mechanism, regardless of which fact is modified. Despite fact-specific weight changes, we argue that ROME and MEMIT target the same subset of weights critical for maintaining edits. To isolate this subset, we train a compact binary mask over the edited weights. The mask reverses 80% of edits on the training set and over 70% on the test set, confirming that diverse edits share a common functional structure. Our analysis reveals that the mask reverses edits by eliminating overattention in later layers. Additionally, we show that injecting the mask during editing drops editing success from 98% to 38%, demonstrating that this mechanism is necessary for edits to succeed. Our finding that edits suppress rather than overwrite knowledge explains why ROME and MEMIT fail to propagate changes to related facts. The identified common functional subspace informs detection and defense against unwanted edits.

</details>


### [94] [Representation Signatures and Risk-Feedback Alignment in LLM Trading Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28850)
*Weicheng Xue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过TradeArena测试平台，系统研究了LLM智能体在金融决策环境中的行为对齐与表征动态，发现失败前可测量的表征收缩信号，验证了结构化风险反馈作为外部对齐信号的有效性，并通过51只股票实验揭示了相关性盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM智能体在金融决策环境中的行为对齐与表征动态，识别市场压力下规划偏移、风险表征分离等失败前兆信号，并验证可审计风险反馈作为外部对齐信号的有效性。

Method: 基于TradeArena交易测试平台，构建80个滚动失败锚点，跨8条LLM轨迹，使用hash、LSA、Transformer及白盒隐藏状态探针进行表征分析；通过无CoT权重、词汇控制、OHLCV噪声、虚假审计报告等压力测试验证鲁棒性；并在51只股票日内交易实验中检验相关性盲点。

Result: 发现可测量的失败前特征：规划嵌入偏离正常质心、融合计划-风险表征区分正常与预回撤状态、流形诊断显示有效秩收缩；压力测试表明理性级收缩依赖推理过程，意图空间收缩仍存在；结构化风险反馈可作为对齐信号但非通用性能增强器；发现LLM理由常为耦合资产集中暴露提供辩护，而风险层反复削减。

Conclusion: 可审计的风险反馈与表征轨迹揭示了LLM金融推理何时对齐、漂移或失败，支持研究性声明而非盈利性声明。

Abstract: We study behavioral alignment and representation dynamics of large language model (LLM) agents in financial decision environments. Using TradeArena, an auditable trading-agent testbed with risk reports, execution simulation, memory, and replayable trajectories, we analyze how rationales, positions, and interventions evolve under market stress. We find measurable pre-failure signatures: planning embeddings drift from normal-state centroids, fused plan-risk representations separate normal from pre-drawdown states, and manifold diagnostics show effective-rank contraction before failures. To address small-sample and embedding-choice concerns, we use 80 rolling failure anchors across eight LLM trajectories and show that contraction persists across hash, LSA, Transformer, and white-box hidden-state probes. Stress tests with CoT-free target weights, lexical controls, OHLCV noise, and false-audit reports indicate that rationale-level contraction can vanish without rationales, while intent-space contraction may remain; lexical diversity does not collapse; and fused signatures remain informative under noise. We also find that structured risk feedback can act as an external alignment signal without fine-tuning, but not as a universal performance enhancer: true audit feedback improves calibration for some models, return and drawdown for others, and reveals cases where hidden or placebo feedback has higher short-horizon return but weaker alignment diagnostics. Finally, a 51-stock intraday experiment reveals a correlation blind spot: LLM rationales often justify concentrated exposure to coupled assets that the risk layer repeatedly clips, with a rolling Markowitz baseline as a covariance reference. These results support a research claim rather than a profitability claim: auditable risk feedback and representation trajectories reveal when LLM financial reasoning is aligning, drifting, or failing.

</details>


### [95] [Mechanistic origins of catastrophic forgetting: why RL preserves circuits better than SFT?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28860)
*Jeanmely Rojas Nunez,Viraj Sawant,Nathan Allen,Nomgondalai Amgalanbaatar,Yannis Zongo,Vasu Sharma,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出差异电路脆弱性指标，从机制层面比较RL和SFT对LLM内部计算电路的影响，发现SFT适应快但破坏电路更严重、遗忘更多，RL则保留更多电路但适应较慢，电路保存程度或可解释RL对灾难性遗忘的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作从行为层面发现RL比SFT更有效地保留先验能力，但缺乏机制层面的解释。本文旨在探究RL的优势是否体现在对内部计算电路的更好保存上。

Method: 提出差异电路脆弱性（differential circuit vulnerability）指标，在注意力头层级度量微调导致电路退化的程度。以Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct为基座模型，在科学问答任务上比较RL与SFT的效果。

Result: SFT对目标任务适应更快，但导致更大的电路破坏和先验能力遗忘；RL保留了更大比例的基座电路，但任务适应速度较慢。存在机制层面的权衡。

Conclusion: 电路保存程度可能是RL在灾难性遗忘上更鲁棒的原因之一，为理解不同微调方法的机制差异提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) frequently induces catastrophic forgetting of prior capabilities. Recent work has shown that reinforcement learning (RL) retains prior capabilities more effectively than supervised fine-tuning (SFT), attributing this to policy-gradient updates remaining closer to the base policy \cite{shenfeld2025rl}. We extend this behavioral account to the mechanistic level and ask whether RL's advantage is mirrored by stronger preservation of internal computational circuits. We introduce differential circuit vulnerability, a head-level measure of how much a circuit degrades under fine-tuning, and use it to compare RL and SFT on Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct adapted to scientific question-answering. We find a clear mechanistic trade-off: SFT adapts more rapidly to the target task but produces substantially greater circuit disruption and forgetting of prior capabilities, whereas RL preserves a larger fraction of the base circuit at the cost of slower task adaptation. These findings suggest that circuit preservation may help explain why RL is more robust to catastrophic forgetting. We released our code here: https://github.com/rl-sft-circuit-research/differential-circuit-vulnerability.

</details>


### [96] [Molecular Lead Optimization via Agentic Tool Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28862)
*Lingxiao Li,Haobo Zhang,Ruohao Fan,Bin Chen,Jiayu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE，一种基于轨迹感知和LLM推理的分子先导优化智能体，将工具选择建模为序列决策问题，实现前瞻性分子优化，在ADMET优化任务中优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有先导优化方法多采用单步分子优化，无法考虑序列设计决策的长期后果，缺乏对优化轨迹的前瞻性规划能力。

Method: 提出TRACE框架，利用LLM推理能力，将分子优化工具的选择建模为序列决策问题；智能体在给定先导分子和优化目标后，基于轨迹感知在动作轨迹上做出决策，在结构约束下实现前瞻性细化。

Result: 在多个ADMET优化任务上，TRACE在优化成功率、性质改善幅度和有效性方面均优于基线模型，同时保持了分子相似性。

Conclusion: 轨迹感知的序列决策方法能够有效提升分子先导优化的性能，为AI辅助药物发现中的先导优化阶段提供了新的解决范式。

Abstract: Drug discovery is a lengthy and resource-intensive process composed of multiple stages. Among these stages, lead optimization plays a critical role in transforming early hit compounds into viable drug candidates. This stage requires improving ADMET-related properties through subtle structural refinement while preserving key molecular substructures responsible for binding affinity to disease targets. Recent advances in artificial intelligence have shown promise in accelerating various aspects of drug discovery; however, most existing approaches to lead optimization rely on one-step molecular optimization, which fail to account for the long-term consequences of sequential design decisions. To address this limitation, we propose TRACE, a trajectory-aware, LLM-reasoning agent for molecular lead optimization that formulates tool selection as a sequential decision-making problem over action trajectories. Given a lead molecule and an optimization objective, TRACE makes trajectory-aware decisions over molecular optimization tools, enabling forward-looking refinement under structural constraints. Experiments on multiple ADMET optimization tasks show that our agent achieves higher optimization success, larger property improvements, and higher validity, while preserving molecular similarity compared to baseline models.

</details>


### [97] [Self-Play Reinforcement Learning under Imperfect Information in Big 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28863)
*Aalok Patwa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在四人非完美信息纸牌游戏Big 2中，通过自监督强化学习框架系统比较了多种算法，发现PPO在多种对手设置下优于MC Q近似、SARSA和Q-learning，并验证了适度熵正则化和当前策略自对弈的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非完美信息多人博弈中，智能体需要在隐藏信息、稀疏奖励和非平稳对手条件下做出决策。现有研究缺乏在可控环境下对多种强化学习算法的系统比较，本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建了自对弈强化学习框架，在统一的环境、输入表征、训练预算和评估协议下，比较了PPO、蒙特卡洛Q近似、SARSA和Q-learning四种算法，并探究了熵正则化和不同自对弈策略（当前策略自对弈、检查点自对弈、固定对手训练）的影响。

Result: PPO在对抗随机、贪心和启发式Big 2对手时均优于其他三种算法；适度熵正则化可防止策略过于确定性而提升性能；当前策略自对弈在有限预算下提供了比检查点自对弈或固定对手训练更优的训练课程。

Conclusion: Big 2是研究非完美信息、多人交互、延迟奖励和可变动作集下深度强化学习的有效受控实验环境。

Abstract: Imperfect-information multiplayer games test whether agents can act under hidden information, sparse rewards, and non-stationary opponents. We study these challenges in Big 2, a four-player imperfect-information card game. We develop a self-play RL framework for Big 2 that enables controlled comparisons between policy-gradient and value-approximating agents. Under a common environment, input representation, training budget, and evaluation protocol, PPO outperforms Monte Carlo Q approximation, SARSA, and Q-learning against random, greedy, and heuristic Big 2 opponents. We further find that moderate entropy regularization improves PPO by preventing the policy from becoming overly deterministic, and that current-policy self-play provides a stronger finite-budget curriculum than checkpoint self-play or fixed-opponent training. Together, these results show that Big 2 is a useful controlled setting for studying deep RL under imperfect information, multiplayer interaction, delayed rewards, and variable action sets.

</details>


### [98] [Emergent Semantic Representations in World Models through Physical Interaction without Linguistic Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28865)
*Jiayi Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现，基于VAE的世界模型在随机具身探索中，其潜空间会自发形成与物理几何结构对应的语义表征，且预测性能与语义对齐共同演化，证实物理世界几何结构是世界模型表征的组织原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在没有语言监督的条件下，世界模型从物理探索中能学到什么，以及其内部表征的组织原则。

Method: 训练基于VAE的世界模型进行随机具身探索，通过方向准确率、位置RSA等指标评估潜空间的几何结构语义对齐，并设置KL正则化（beta）的双重敲除实验验证预测性能与语义对齐的共变关系。

Result: 世界模型潜空间发展出镜像物理几何的空间语义结构（方向准确率0.677±0.029 vs 随机编码器0.547；位置RSA提升6.6倍）；预测性能与语义对齐显著共相关（Spearman r=-0.61, p=0.004）；标准KL正则化（beta=0.1）同时破坏两者性能，降低beta至0.001则同时恢复。

Conclusion: 物理世界几何结构是世界模型表征的核心组织原则，对设计具有语义基础的具身智能体有直接启示。

Abstract: What does a world model learn from physical exploration, without any linguistic supervision? We argue the answer is organized by a single principle: the geometric structure of the physical world. Training a VAE-based world model on random embodied exploration, we find that its latent space develops spatial semantic structure that mirrors physical geometry -- direction accuracy 0.677+-0.029 versus 0.547 for a randomly initialized encoder, and position RSA 0.192+-0.047 versus 0.029 for random encoders (6.6x improvement), showing that training induces genuine structural organization beyond CNN inductive bias. Across 20 temporal checkpoints, prediction performance and semantic alignment co-improve (Spearman r=-0.61, p=0.004), consistent with the shared-driver account. We confirm this through a double knockout: standard KL regularization (beta=0.1) forces the encoder away from geometric structure, and both prediction performance and semantic alignment collapse simultaneously to near-chance by step 50,000 -- exactly as the shared-driver account predicts. Reducing beta to 0.001 restores geometric access and recovers both capabilities together. These findings establish physical world geometry as the organizing principle of world model representations, with direct implications for the design of semantically grounded embodied agents.

</details>


### [99] [Continuity and Ordinality Matter: Constraining Time Series Tokens for Effective Time Series Analysis with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28866)
*Musheng Li,Ziying Zhang,Cheng jin,Yuantao Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出COM策略，通过在初始化和训练阶段引入几何约束来保留时间序列token的连续性和序数性，从而提升基于token的时间序列大语言模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于token的时间序列大语言模型忽视了时间序列token固有的连续性和序数性，这严重限制了模型性能。

Method: 提出COM（Continuity and Ordinality Matter）策略，在token嵌入的初始化和训练阶段集成几何约束，以保留时间序列token的连续性和序数性。

Result: 在多个时间序列分析基准上的实验结果表明，COM一致性地提升了基于token的TS-LLM性能，取得了具有竞争力的结果和强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 保留时间序列token嵌入中的连续性和序数性对于基于token的TS-LLM的有效性至关重要，COM策略是一种有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Token-based time series large language models (TS-LLMs) have emerged as a promising direction for time series analysis and reasoning. However, prior studies largely overlook the inherent continuity and ordinality of time series tokens, which substantially limits model performance. In this paper, we argue that preserving these properties in time series token embeddings is crucial for the effectiveness of token-based TS-LLMs. To this end, we propose COM (Continuity and Ordinality Matter), a continuity- and ordinality-aware strategy that integrates geometric constraints into both the initialization and training stages. Empirical results on multiple time series analysis benchmarks demonstrate that COM consistently improves the performance of token-based TS-LLMs, achieving competitive results and strong generalizability. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/COM .

</details>


### [100] [PrismFlow: Residual Dynamics for Flow Matching in Time-Series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28867)
*Junru Zhang,Lang Feng,Jinbo Wang,Xu Guo,Yucheng Wang,Han Yu,Min Wu,Yabo Dong,Duanqing Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PrismFlow提出了一种基于Koopman动力学的流匹配方法，通过多个专家模块学习残差动态校正，并采用置信度感知的胜者全得（WTA）目标训练，以解决标准流匹配中单一全局估计器在多模态多尺度时序数据上产生的频谱收缩和模式覆盖不足问题，在多个基准上取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的时间序列数据常呈现多模态模式和多尺度动力学（包括振荡和高频变化），而标准流匹配（Flow Matching）依赖于单一有限容量的全局向量场估计器。当不同动态分支经过相近流状态但需要不兼容的条件速度时，单一估计器会学习过度平滑的局部输运场近似，导致分支特异性动态衰减、频谱失真和模式覆盖不足。

Method: 提出PrismFlow方法：1）引入Koopman启发的动态专家模块，每个专家在潜在空间中学习残差校正，将局部非线性时间演化近似为线性转移；2）提出置信度感知的胜者全得（WTA）目标，仅更新与每个样本最对齐的专家，同时对其他专家的梯度进行掩码，鼓励模态特异性专业化；3）在采样时，所选专家向全局输运场添加残差动态校正，在保持流匹配稳定性的同时恢复细粒度高频时间结构。

Result: 在多个基准上，PrismFlow有效缓解了标准流匹配中的频谱收缩问题，取得了SOTA性能：Context-FID提升15.6%，判别分数（Discriminative Score）改进38.6%，在低数据场景下保持鲁棒性，并有效支持预测和插补任务。

Conclusion: PrismFlow通过Koopman动态专家与WTA训练策略，成功解决了标准流匹配在多模态多尺度时序生成中的频谱失真问题，验证了专家专业化校正对于提升生成质量的必要性。

Abstract: Generating high-quality time-series data is challenging because real-world signals often exhibit multimodal patterns and multiscale dynamics, including oscillations and high-frequency variations. Flow Matching (FM) offers an efficient alternative to diffusion models, but practical implementations typically rely on a single finite-capacity global vector-field estimator. In such heterogeneous temporal distributions, distinct regimes may pass through nearby flow states while requiring incompatible conditional velocities. A monolithic estimator trained with the standard $\ell_2$ velocity-matching objective may therefore learn an overly smoothed approximation of the local transport field. This estimator-level smoothing can attenuate branch-specific dynamics, leading to spectral distortion and poor mode coverage. To address this, we propose PrismFlow, a new FM method with Koopman-inspired dynamical experts. Each expert learns residual corrections in a latent space where local nonlinear temporal evolution can be approximated by linear transitions. We further propose a confidence-aware Winner-Take-All (WTA) objective that updates only the expert best aligned with each sample while masking gradients to the others, encouraging mode-specific specialization. During sampling, the selected expert adds a residual dynamical correction to the global transport field, preserving FM stability while recovering fine-grained and high-frequency temporal structures. Across various benchmarks, PrismFlow effectively mitigates the spectral contraction in standard FM and achieves state-of-the-art performance, with a 15.6% gain in Context-FID and a 38.6% improvement in Discriminative Score, while remaining robust in low-data settings and effective for forecasting and imputation.

</details>


### [101] [Balancing Multimodal Learning through Label Space Reshaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28869)
*Xiaoyu Ma,Weijie Zhang,Yuanhao Gao,Han Miao,Yongjian Deng,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多模态学习中模态不平衡问题（不同模态收敛速度差异导致优化失衡），本文从标签侧提出首个平衡方法BMLR，通过重塑跨模态标签空间来均衡各模态的映射难度，从而提升多模态性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要从加强弱模态或调整优化梯度入手，但仅补偿优化速率差异，未从模态层面分析学习速度差异的根源，且往往以牺牲强模态的优化能力为代价。

Method: 提出Balanced Multimodal Label Reshaping (BMLR)，从标签侧重塑跨模态标签空间，使不同模态到共享标签空间的映射难度均衡化，从而促进模态交互并为各模态注入更丰富的类间信息。

Result: 跨多种架构的广泛实验表明，BMLR持续提升多模态性能，并与多种模型设计具有强兼容性。

Conclusion: 多模态学习中的学习速度差异源于各模态特征空间到共享标签空间映射难度的不同，通过标签侧设计均衡映射难度是一种有效且兼容性强的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal learning often suffers from modality imbalance, where modalities that converge faster dominate optimization while others remain undertrained. Existing approaches typically mitigate this issue by strengthening the weak modality or adjusting optimization gradients. However, such strategies mainly compensate for optimization rate discrepancies, often at the expense of the strong modality's optimization capacity, without analyzing how these discrepancies arise at the modality level. Based on theoretical insights and empirical observations, we argue that the discrepancy of learning pace arises from differences in the mapping difficulty between modality-specific feature space and the shared label space. To address this issue, we propose Balanced Multimodal Label Reshaping (BMLR), the first method that promotes multimodal balance from the label-side design. BMLR reshapes the cross-modal label space to equalize mapping difficulty across modalities, thereby facilitating modality interaction and injecting richer inter-class information into each modality. Extensive experiments across multiple architectures demonstrate that BMLR consistently improves multimodal performance and exhibits strong compatibility with diverse model designs. The source code will be released soon.

</details>


### [102] [Representation Alignment Rests on Linear Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28870)
*Kiril Bangachev,Guy Bresler,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过信号、偏置和噪声三元统计框架研究柏拉图式表征假说（PRH），提出普拉顿式对齐源于对象-属性的线性表征关系（LRH），并通过稀疏自编码器、中心化归一化、以及词频与对齐度的正相关性等实验验证了这一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究为什么不同架构和训练方式下模型会收敛到对齐的表征（柏拉图式表征假说），并理解其背后的统计机制。

Method: 提出三元统计框架：（1）信号——基于线性表征假说（LRH），用稀疏自编码器提取线性对象-属性特征；（2）偏置——通过中心化和归一化缓解不同架构带来的隐式偏置差异；（3）噪声——通过词频与对齐度的相关性分析验证有限样本训练导致的表征噪声源于数据稀缺性。

Result: 稀疏表征比稠密表征展现出更强的跨模态对齐；中心化和归一化能一致性地改善跨模型对齐；词频与对齐度之间存在强且一致的正相关关系。

Conclusion: 提出了一个精炼线性表征假说的统计模型，能够解释现代多样化AI架构中表征对齐的更多现象。

Abstract: We investigate the Platonic Representation Hypothesis (PRH) through a tripartite statistical framework of representations: signal, bias, and noise. {1) Signal:} We propose that Platonic alignment arises from the universal relationship between objects and attributes, which is encoded linearly in representations according to the Linear Representation Hypothesis (LRH). We provide evidence that LRH helps explain PRH by extracting linear object-attribute features with sparse autoencoders and showing that these sparse representations often exhibit stronger cross-modal alignment than their dense counterparts. {2) Bias:} Models have different implicit biases due to the diverse architectures and training procedures used. We show that this difference can be partially mitigated. Centering and normalization consistently improve cross-model alignment. {3) Noise:} Finite-sample training leads to noise in representations. We provide evidence that representational noise is driven by data scarcity by revealing a strong and consistent positive correlation between word frequency and alignment in LLMs and text embedding models. Synthesizing signal, bias, and noise, we propose a statistical model that refines the Linear Representation Hypothesis and explains further phenomena related to the alignment of representations emerging from diverse modern AI architectures.

</details>


### [103] [Pre-Registering the Detectable Effect: A Paired-MDE Budget for 4-bit Quantization Benchmarks, with a Pilot Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28873)
*Zexin Zhuang,Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种将经典配对二项式样本量计算方法（Miettinen, 1968）适配到量化基准测试中的保守最小可检测效应（MDE）界，并通过对四个模型和四个基准的未配对先导审计及MMLU提示模板研究，证明了基准方差主要源于二项式采样噪声，而非模型量化本身的不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化基准测试中，研究者常因基准方差而难以判断量化方法间性能差异的真实性。当前缺乏明确的方法论框架来区分基准噪声与量化噪声，本文旨在将量化声明的可靠性问题转化为基准设计者可在实验前承诺的预算线。

Method: (1) 将Miettinen配对二项式样本量计算适配到量化基准，推导保守MDE界 $\delta^{*} \le (z_{1-\alpha/2}+z_{1-\beta})\sqrt{\rho_d/m}$，其中$m$为配对项目数，$\rho_d$为FP16-NF4不一致率；(2) 对四个模型与四个基准进行$k=5$折、$n=100$的未配对先导审计；(3) 并行MMLU提示模板研究，比较量化噪声尺度与提示噪声尺度；(4) 提供五行的预注册模板。

Result: (1) 假设$\rho_d=0.10$（未测量的规划值），所有观察到的NF4-FP16差值均低于MDE；(2) 大部分跨折标准差落在$\pm 1.5$个百分点以内，与二项式参考$\sqrt{p(1-p)/n}$一致，表明$n=100$子样本中的"基准不可靠性"方差主要是二项式采样噪声；(3) OPT-WinoGrande边界案例（$|\Delta|=3.2$ pp）在$\rho_d=0.10$下低于MDE，但在$\rho_d=0.05$下高于MDE；(4) MMLU提示模板范围为2-10 pp，达到或超过最大量化差值（3.2 pp）。

Conclusion: 量化审计需先固定提示模板，否则模板方差会被吸收进噪声基底；所提MDE界可为基准设计者提供预先承诺的预算线，量化声明应基于该框架进行评估；提供了五线预注册模板以推广该实践。

Abstract: This is a planning-method note with an unpaired pilot audit. We adapt the classical paired-binary sample-size calculation (Miettinen, 1968) to quantization benchmarks, giving a conservative minimum detectable effect (MDE) bound $δ^{*} \le (z_{1-α/2}+z_{1-β})\sqrt{ρ_d/m}$ in the paired item count $m$ and the FP16-NF4 disagreement rate $ρ_d$. The bound turns "how reliable is my quantization claim?" into a one-line budget a benchmark designer can commit to before running. We illustrate the bound on four models and four benchmarks ($k=5$ splits of $n=100$), and add a parallel MMLU prompt-template study to put the bound's quantization-noise scale alongside the prompt-noise scale. Assuming $ρ_d=0.10$ (an unmeasured planning value), all observed NF4-FP16 deltas fall below the implied MDE, and most cross-split SDs lie within $\pm 1.5$ pp of the binomial reference $\sqrt{p(1-p)/n}$, so much of the variance reported as "benchmark unreliability" on $n=100$ subsamples is binomial sampling noise. The single borderline cell (OPT-WinoGrande, $|Δ|=3.2$ pp) is below the implied MDE at $ρ_d=0.10$ but above it at $ρ_d=0.05$, illustrating the planning trade-off the bound makes explicit. On MMLU, prompt-template ranges of 2-10 pp meet or exceed the largest observed quantization delta (3.2 pp), so a quantization audit that does not first fix the prompt template absorbs template variance into its noise floor. We complement the bound with a five-line pre-registration template.

</details>


### [104] [Context Distillation as Latent Memory Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28889)
*Ziyang Zheng,Zeju Li,Xiangyu Wen,Jianyuan Zhong,Junhua Huang,Lei Chen,Mingxuan Yuan,Qiang Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将上下文蒸馏形式化为潜在记忆管理问题，通过将每个上下文蒸馏为独立LoRA适配器构建模块化记忆库，并引入Self-Gating机制决定是否激活潜在记忆，结合缓存共享提升推理效率。实验表明该方法显著优于基线，且Self-Gating通过停用不必要记忆增强了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文蒸馏方法忽略了多个蒸馏出的潜在记忆应如何存储、检索以及在非理想环境下安全激活的问题。

Method: 1) 将每个上下文蒸馏为独立LoRA适配器，构建模块化记忆库；2) 根据查询检索候选记忆，路由至最适配器；3) 使用Self-Gating机制决定是否激活潜在记忆；4) 引入缓存共享降低推理开销。

Result: 该方法在检索场景下显著优于基线，Self-Gating通过停用不必要的潜在记忆提升了鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 上下文蒸馏可视为潜在记忆管理问题，模块化记忆库加Self-Gating机制能有效解决多记忆的存储、检索和安全激活问题。

Abstract: Context distillation compresses contextual information into model parameters, yet existing methods often ignore how multiple distilled latent memories should be stored, retrieved, and safely activated in non-oracle settings. We formulate context distillation as a latent memory management problem. We distill each context into an independent LoRA adapter, forming a modular memory bank that enables explicit memory selection. Given a query, our framework retrieves candidate memories, routes the query to the most suitable adapter, and uses a Self-Gating mechanism to decide whether latent memory should be activated. To improve efficiency, we further introduce cache sharing to reduce management overhead during inference. Experiments show that our method substantially outperforms baselines with retrieval, while Self-Gating improves robustness by deactivate unnecessary latent memories.

</details>


### [105] [Feature Geometry of LoRA Adapters: A Sparse Autoencoder Analysis of Representational Divergence in Fine-Tuned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28896)
*Prasanth K K*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过稀疏自编码器研究LoRA微调对大型语言模型内部表征几何结构的影响，发现LoRA诱导的特征与预训练特征之间存在弱几何对齐，且LoRA更新占据了残差流中部分独特的表征结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA微调已被广泛采用，但其对模型内部表征的具体影响尚不明确，理解这些变化对模型的可解释性、安全审计和适应分析至关重要。

Method: 提出delta激活框架隔离适配器对残差流的贡献；在Gemma-2-9B上使用秩4/8/16/32的LoRA训练适配器专属SAE；通过余弦相似度、主角度分析和中心核对齐（CKA）对比学习到的特征空间与预训练SAE字典。

Result: LoRA诱导的特征字典与预训练SAE特征之间的几何对齐较弱；适配器专属SAE能更有效地重建delta激活；特征密度随秩和深度增加而增加，几何差异在不同秩下保持相对稳定。

Conclusion: LoRA微调可以诱导出预训练可解释性字典无法完全捕捉的特征结构，这对微调语言模型的机制可解释性、适应分析及安全审计具有重要启示。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as a widely adopted approach for adapting large language models, yet the internal representational changes induced by LoRA fine-tuning remain insufficiently understood. In this work, we investigate the geometry of LoRA-induced representations using Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs). We introduce a delta activation framework that isolates the adapter-specific contribution to the residual stream.
  Using Gemma-2-9B with LoRA ranks 4, 8, 16, and 32, we train adapter-specific SAEs across multiple transformer layers and compare their learned feature spaces with pretrained SAE dictionaries. We evaluate representational alignment using cosine similarity between decoder directions, principal-angle analysis of feature subspaces, and Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) between activation representations.
  Across layers and ranks, we consistently observe comparatively weak geometric alignment between LoRA-induced feature dictionaries and pretrained SAE features. Adapter-specific SAEs also reconstruct delta activations more effectively than pretrained SAEs, suggesting that LoRA updates occupy partially distinct representational structure within the residual stream. Additionally, feature density increases with rank and depth, while geometric divergence remains relatively stable across ranks.
  These findings provide empirical evidence that LoRA fine-tuning can induce feature structures that are not fully captured by pretrained interpretability dictionaries, with implications for mechanistic interpretability, adaptation analysis, and safety auditing of fine-tuned language models.

</details>


### [106] [Spectral Guidance for Flexible and Efficient Control of Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28900)
*Gabriel Moreira,Manuel Marques,João Paulo Costeira,Chenyan Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Spectral Guidance框架，利用生成过程的固有几何结构对扩散模型进行高效控制，无需重新训练或反向传播，在CIFAR-10上将条件准确率提升37个百分点，采样速度提升4倍，并揭示了有效引导的最优时间窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型控制方法依赖于重新训练或对降噪器进行反向传播，效率低下且不稳定。本文旨在利用生成过程的固有几何结构，设计一种无需重训练和反向传播的高效、稳定控制框架。

Method: 将条件期望算子的奇异函数作为信息量最大的特征，通过自监督目标学习该基；然后将任意引导信号（标签、CLIP嵌入、掩码等）投影到该基上，直接作用于采样轨迹，实现无需降噪器反向传播的控制。

Result: 在CIFAR-10上，条件准确率比最强的无训练基线提升37个百分点，同时采样速度提升4倍；同一套表示同时支持标签、CLIP和空间（掩码）引导，无需辅助模型。

Conclusion: 该框架揭示了生成过程中的相变现象，精确指出了有效引导的最优时间窗口，为扩散模型的高效、多模态控制提供了统一且理论坚实的解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce Spectral Guidance, a framework for controlling diffusion models by leveraging the intrinsic geometry of the generative process. As data is progressively corrupted by noise, only a small number of features remain informative for control. We characterize them as the singular functions of a conditional expectation operator and show that they can be learned via a self-supervised objective. Once recovered, this basis enables the projection of arbitrary guidance signals, such as labels, CLIP embeddings, or masks, directly onto the sampling trajectory. This approach allows for stable, high-fidelity control without retraining or denoiser backpropagation during sampling. Empirically, we improve conditional accuracy on CIFAR-10 by 37 percentage points over the strongest training-free baseline while offering $4\times$ faster sampling. Moreover, the same representations that support label and CLIP guidance also enable spatial control, such as mask-based guidance, without auxiliary models. Finally, our framework reveals a phase transition in the generative process, pinpointing the optimal time window for effective guidance.

</details>


### [107] [When LLM Reward Design Fails: Diagnostic-Driven Refinement for Sparse Structured RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28918)
*Youting Wang,Yuan Tang,Bowen Liu,Xuan Liu,Dingyan Shang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将LLM生成奖励塑形（Reward Shaping）视为调试（Debugging）而非一次性生成（One-shot Generation），并通过诊断驱动的迭代优化显著提升了稀疏、结构化强化学习任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成奖励函数的一次性方法存在两种主要失败模式（奖励洪水与语义/API误解）以及一种较罕见的弱塑形情况，需要更系统化的调试框架来改进奖励函数生成质量。

Method: 提出诊断驱动迭代优化方法：利用训练诊断和失败模式分类法指导目标性的奖励函数修订。在MiniGrid（核心评估）和MuJoCo（边界压力测试）上使用PPO训练智能体，与指标仅重提示、静态词汇表控制、预算匹配和Best-of-3等对照组进行比较。

Result: DoorKey-8x8从2.3%提升至97.6%，KeyCorridor从31.2%提升至86.7%（种子间方差大）；静态词汇表控制恢复了大部分差距（87.6%；70.7%），表明分类提示是主要机制；连续控制任务中，基于成功率的诊断在密集奖励环境可能失效。

Conclusion: 该方法适用于具有可靠接口的稀疏结构化任务和PPO算法；分类法提示是主要改进机制，动态标签仅提供部分增量证据；成本优势体现在低调用协议而非与种群搜索的基准比较；在交叉方差设计的四个环境中，点估计表明LLM奖励函数方差主导时增益更大，但bootstrap区间较宽。

Abstract: For sparse, structured reinforcement-learning tasks with semantic reward-function interfaces, LLM-generated reward shaping is better framed as debugging than one-shot generation. We study PPO-trained agents using MiniGrid as core evaluation and MuJoCo as boundary stress test. Our audit finds two dominant one-shot failure modes -- reward flooding and semantic/API misunderstanding -- plus a rarer weak-shaping case. We propose diagnostic-driven iterative refinement, where training diagnostics and a failure-mode taxonomy guide targeted reward-function revision. Refinement improves DoorKey-8x8 from 2.3% to 97.6% and KeyCorridor from 31.2% to 86.7% with high seed-to-seed variance. Controls show these gains are not from retrying or extra training: metrics-only re-prompting yields large drops, while a static-vocabulary control recovers much of the gap (87.6%; 70.7%), showing the taxonomy prompt is a major mechanism and dynamic labels provide only partially isolated incremental evidence. Budget-matched and Best-of-3 comparisons separate refinement from selection and training-time effects. Component-removal tests, sensitivity analyses, and an audit against author labels provide converging evidence for the debugging interpretation while revealing calibration limits. Continuous-control results show the boundary: success-based diagnostics can misfire in dense-reward locomotion, and return-trend feedback removes one false-positive mechanism without robust gains. The low-call protocol is a cost contrast with population-based reward search, not a benchmark comparison. In four crossed-variance-design environments, point estimates suggest larger gains when LLM reward-function variance dominates but bootstrap intervals are wide. The method is bounded to sparse structured tasks with reliable interfaces under PPO; fields like event_text may help, hurt, or be neutral.

</details>


### [108] [CosmicFish-HRM: Adaptive Reasoning via Hierarchical Recurrent Mechanisms in Compact Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28919)
*Venkat Akhil Lakkapragada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CosmicFish-HRM 是一个紧凑型语言模型，通过层级推理模块（HRM）动态分配推理深度，在推理时根据输入复杂度自适应调整计算量，为替代单纯增加参数量提升推理能力提供了新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型虽推理能力强，但往往依赖海量参数和高昂推理成本。本文探索一种不同的方向：在紧凑模型中实现自适应推理深度，以更高效的方式提升推理能力。

Method: 提出 CosmicFish-HRM 模型，核心是层级推理模块（HRM），在推理时动态执行高层和低层推理循环，根据输入复杂度自适应决定何时停止。模型还集成了 Grouped Query Attention、RoPE 和 SwiGLU 等现代 Transformer 组件。

Result: 模型学会了非均匀的推理行为，能根据不同任务和输入分配不同的推理步数。在小规模下额外推理基础设施带来开销，但作者假设随着模型规模增长，HRM 核心的相对成本会下降，该权衡将变得有利。

Conclusion: 自适应推理深度可能是替代单纯依赖参数规模来提升推理能力的有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved strong reasoning capabilities, though often at the cost of massive parameter counts and expensive inference. In this work, we explore a different direction: adaptive reasoning depth in compact language models. We present CosmicFish-HRM, a compact language model built around a Hierarchical Reasoning Module (HRM) that dynamically allocates computational effort during inference. Instead of applying fixed computation to every input, the model iterates through high-level and low-level reasoning cycles and learns when to halt based on input complexity. CosmicFish-HRM combines this adaptive reasoning core with modern transformer components including Grouped Query Attention, RoPE, and SwiGLU activations. While the additional reasoning infrastructure introduces overhead at small scale, we hypothesize that this tradeoff becomes increasingly favorable as model size grows and the relative cost of the HRM core diminishes. Our results show that the model learns non-uniform reasoning behavior, allocating different numbers of reasoning steps across tasks and inputs. These findings suggest that adaptive reasoning depth may offer a promising alternative to relying solely on parameter scale for reasoning capability.

</details>


### [109] [A Training-Time Diagnostic for Generalization via the Log-Alignment Ratio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28975)
*Ali Shehper,Ashish Vaswani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了对数比对率（LAR），将其重新表述为权重谱与激活谱之间的重叠度量，并证明LAR能够追踪记忆与泛化之间的转换，在grokking中预测学习函数的有效维度，在语言模型预训练中追踪泛化差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化理论中引入的对数比对率（LAR）此前缺乏清晰的数学解释和计算框架，作者希望揭示其本质并探索其监控模型泛化行为的实用性。

Method: 将LAR重新定义为权重谱p（归一化平方奇异值的分布）与激活谱q（输入在奇异方向上的归一化平方投影分布）之间的重叠度量；通过grokking和3B参数语言模型预训练两个场景验证其有效性。

Result: 在grokking中，LAR能预测学习函数的有效维度k ≈ n^(2(1-LAR))；在3B参数语言模型预训练中，LAR偏离无过拟合基线的程度追踪泛化差距，且下降速率在过拟合临近时增加。

Conclusion: LAR是一种计算开销可忽略、无需验证数据的有效泛化监控指标，能够追踪记忆与泛化的转换过程。

Abstract: We study the log-alignment ratio (LAR), a measure of parameter-activation alignment, introduced in parameterization theory. We reformulate it as the overlap between a weight spectrum $p$ of the normalized squared singular values of a matrix and an activation spectrum $q$ of the normalized squared projections of inputs onto its singular directions. We show that unembedding LAR tracks the transition between memorization and generalization in two different settings by capturing the spread of $p$ and $q$ during training. In grokking, LAR predicts the effective dimension of the learned function: $k \approx n^{2(1-\text{LAR})}$, where $n$ is the input dimension of the matrix. In 3B-parameter language model pre-training, its deviation from a non-overfitting baseline tracks the generalization gap, and its rate of decline increases as overfitting approaches. LAR is computable from quantities available during the forward pass with negligible computational overhead, and requires no held-out validation data.

</details>


### [110] [Comparing Post-Hoc Explainable AI Methods for Interpreting Black-Box EEG Models in Depression Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28977)
*Antonia Šarčević,Nikolina Frid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多种事后可解释性方法（DeepSHAP、Integrated Gradients、GradCAM、Occlusion、Permutation Feature Importance）在基于InceptionTime架构的EEG抑郁症检测模型中的应用，发现不同方法在额叶、颞叶和后部脑区（尤其是右半球）存在部分收敛的归因模式，但方法间的变异性也凸显了假设依赖问题。研究强调事后可解释性在精神科EEG分类器中的用途与局限性，属于探索性分析而非临床生物标志物证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在基于EEG的抑郁症检测中表现优异，但高容量模型的决策过程难以解释，缺乏对模型内部机制的理解限制了其临床可信度和应用。

Method: 采用InceptionTime架构进行EEG-MDD分类，在受试者级别分层5折交叉验证框架下，应用五种事后可解释性方法（DeepSHAP、Integrated Gradients、GradCAM、Occlusion、Permutation Feature Importance），通过全局归因聚合在EEG片段和受试者层面进行分析。

Result: 不同可解释性方法揭示了部分收敛的归因模式，额叶、颞叶和后部脑区（尤其是右半球）被反复强调。梯度和扰动方法之间具有显著一致性，而DeepSHAP产生明显不同的归因分布。方法间的变异性凸显了方法论假设对解释结果的影响。

Conclusion: 不同事后可解释性方法捕捉到EEG深度学习模型中部分重叠的相关性结构，观察到的归因模式与先前EEG-MDD研究大致一致，但分析应视为探索性而非神经生理学生物标志物或临床适用性的证据。研究展示了事后可解释性在解释精神科黑盒EEG分类器中的用途与局限性。

Abstract: Recent advances in deep learning have enabled increasingly accurate electroencephalography (EEG)-based classification of Major Depressive Disorder (MDD), but the decision-making processes of high-capacity models remain difficult to interpret. This study investigates multiple post-hoc explainability methods applied to an InceptionTime architecture trained for EEG-based MDD detection. The analysis includes Shapley-based, gradient-based, and perturbation-based attribution approaches: DeepSHAP, Integrated Gradients, GradCAM, Occlusion, and Permutation Feature Importance. Explainability analysis was performed within a subject-level stratified 5-fold cross-validation framework using global attribution aggregation across EEG segments and subjects. The evaluated methods revealed partially convergent attribution patterns, with recurring emphasis on frontal, temporal, and posterior EEG regions, particularly in the right hemisphere. Quantitative comparison demonstrated substantial agreement between gradient- and perturbation-based approaches, while DeepSHAP produced comparatively distinct attribution distributions. At the same time, variability between explainability methods highlighted the influence of methodological assumptions on the resulting explanations. Overall, the results suggest that different post-hoc explainability approaches capture partially overlapping relevance structures in EEG-based deep learning models for depression detection. Although the observed attribution patterns are broadly consistent with several previous EEG studies of MDD, the analysis should be interpreted as exploratory rather than evidence of definitive neurophysiological biomarkers or clinical applicability. The study highlights both the usefulness and limitations of post-hoc explainability for interpreting black-box EEG classifiers in psychiatric applications.

</details>


### [111] [Learning Robust and Task-Invariant Functional Representation from fMRI through Siamese Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28990)
*Jiyao Wang,Peiyu Duan,Nicha C. Dvornek,Lawrence H. Staib,Denis Sukhodolsky,Pamela Ventola,James S. Duncan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BrainSimSiam是一种轻量级自监督学习框架，利用正样本对从fMRI数据中学习鲁棒且通用的特征，在多个下游分类和回归任务中表现优异，接近大规模模型性能，特别适用于样本量有限的神经影像应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: fMRI数据获取成本高、精神科评分量表主观性强导致数据集样本量小且标签质量不稳定，加上fMRI数据高维特性，模型过拟合风险显著增加。

Method: 提出BrainSimSiam，一种轻量级自监督表示学习框架，仅利用正样本对进行学习，无需负样本。

Result: 学习到的表示在多个下游分类和回归任务中表现强劲，超越全监督基线，性能接近大规模模型。

Conclusion: BrainSimSiam为数据有限的神经影像应用提供了高效的自监督表示学习方案，降低了计算资源需求同时保持优异的下游任务性能。

Abstract: Functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) is a powerful tool for investigating human brain function. However, the high cost of data acquisition and the inherent subjectivity of psychiatric rating scales often lead to datasets with small sample sizes and variable label quality, especially when targeting a specific neurological condition. Combined with the inherently high dimensionality of fMRI data, these limitations substantially increase the risk of model overfitting. Recent years have seen growing interest in developing fMRI foundation models by combining multiple datasets; however, the computational resources needed for pretraining and fine-tuning are often prohibitive.
