Extensive experiments on 14 real-world benchmarks demonstrate that DSRD consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance on both link prediction and node classification tasks, with strong generalization across transductive and inductive settings.

</details>


### [148] [A Full-Pipeline Framework for Evaluating Membership Inference Attacks in Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29454)
*Ding Chen,Xinwen Cheng,Xuyang Zhong,Xinping Chen,Xiaolin Huang,Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个系统化的评估框架，用于全面分析不同上下文因素（数据、架构、算法、后训练模块）对成员推理攻击（MIA）效能的影响，并形式化了两种威胁模型，通过三种互补指标（平衡准确率、低FPR下的TPR、低FNR下的TNR）进行公平基准测试，最终提供实用指南和审计工具包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MIA研究缺乏系统化框架来评估不同上下文对MIA效能的影响，导致实践中部署的算法可能在基准测试中表现良好，但在面对真实数据集的具体细节时统计上无效。

Method: 提出了一个覆盖机器学习全流程（数据、架构、算法、后训练模块）的综合评估框架；形式化了两种标准化威胁模型以适配不同的攻击者能力假设；采用三种互补指标（平衡准确率、低FPR下的TPR、低FNR下的TNR）以适应不同的误分类成本场景。

Result: 大量实证评估表明，特定MIA方法的效能对假定的威胁模型和选择的评估指标高度敏感。

Conclusion: 将研究发现提炼为可操作的指南，并提供了一个可直接使用的审计工具包，帮助从业者进行更好的隐私评估。

Abstract: While Membership Inference Attacks (MIAs) are the prevailing method for identifying training data, their application has expanded into privacy auditing and machine unlearning. Nevertheless, the field lacks a systematic framework for evaluating how different contexts affect MIA efficacy. Without such a characterization, practitioners risk deploying algorithms that perform well on benchmarks but become statistically irrelevant when faced with the nuances of specific, real-world datasets. To bridge this gap and provide actionable insights, we introduce a comprehensive evaluation framework that systematically characterizes privacy risks across the entire machine learning pipeline, spanning data, architectures, algorithms, and post-training modules. Designed to inherently capture diverse operational contexts, our framework rigorously evaluates state-of-the-art MIAs across a broad spectrum of training configurations. To account for varying misclassification costs in real-world deployments, we employ three complementary metrics: Balanced Accuracy for symmetric costs, alongside TPR at low FPR (or TNR at low FNR) for asymmetric scenarios where false alarms or missed detections are strictly penalized. Furthermore, recognizing that existing MIAs assume divergent adversary capabilities, we formalize two standardized threat models and adapt these attacks into corresponding variants to ensure an equitable benchmark. Extensive empirical evaluations demonstrate that the efficacy of specific MIA methodologies is highly sensitive to the assumed threat models and chosen evaluation metrics. Ultimately, we distill these findings into actionable guidelines and provide a ready-to-use auditing toolkit, empowering practitioners to conduct better privacy assessments.

</details>


### [149] [Honest Lying: Understanding Memory Confabulation in Reflexive Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29463)
*Prakhar Dixit,Sadia Kamal,Tim Oates*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 反思型智能体依赖自我生成的反思作为记忆，但研究发现这种自我诊断可能失败：智能体会存储对任务的错误解读并持续依赖这些错误信息，即使环境已正确重置。作者提出记忆幻觉（memory confabulation）概念，引入反思重复率（RRR）指标检测问题，并用程序化失败信号提取替代开放式自我诊断，显著提升了正确目标提及率和环境解决率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反思型智能体（Reflexion-style agents）假设智能体能准确诊断自身失败，但这一假设可能系统性失效，导致智能体重复依赖错误信念而无法修正。

Method: 1) 提出记忆幻觉（memory confabulation）概念；2) 设计反思重复率（RRR）指标，基于日志检测对错误反思内容的重复依赖；3) 用程序化轨迹级失败信号提取替代开放式自我诊断作为缓解方案。

Result: 在ALFWorld中识别16个冻结环境（0/121条反思提及正确目标），HumanEval中发现4个类似案例。缓解方案将正确目标提及率从0%提升至86%，RRR从0.64降至0.10，并解决了16个冻结环境中的3个。

Conclusion: 反思性记忆可能强化而非纠正错误信念，程序化故障信号提取比开放式自我诊断更有效。

Abstract: Reflexion-style agents rely on self-generated reflections as memory, implicitly assuming that agents can accurately diagnose their own failures.We show that this assumption can fail systematically: across ALFWorld and HumanEval, agents store confident but incorrect interpretations of the task and continue acting on them across trials,even though the environment resets to the correct task each time. We call this failure mode memory confabulation and introduce the Reflection Repetition Rate (RRR), a log-based metric that detects repeated reliance on incorrect reflective content.Using RRR, we identify 16 frozen environments in ALFWorld, where 0 of 121 reflections mention the correct target object, and 4 analogous cases in HumanEval. Our mitigation replaces open-ended self-diagnosis with programmatic extraction of trajectory-level failure signals, increasing correct object mention from 0% to 86%, reducing RRR from 0.64 to 0.10, and solving 3 of 16 frozen ALFWorld environments, suggesting that reflective memory can reinforce false beliefs rather than correct them.

</details>


### [150] [Composing Non-Conjugate Factor Graphs with Closed-Form Variational Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29467)
*Mykola Lukashchuk,Kyrylo Yemets,Wouter M. Kouw,Dmitry Bagaev,İsmail Şenöz,Jeff Beck,Bert de Vries*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一组因子图原语（双线性因子、指数链接、Gamma先验、高斯似然和等式节点），证明由这些原语组成的任何模型在平均场分解下都能保持闭式变分消息传递，并通过堆叠路由层实现任意决策树的编码，从而建立具有闭式推理的通用函数逼近能力。在时间序列预测中，该方法实现了贝叶斯混合专家模型，门控函数通过推理而非学习获得，在五个基准数据集上提供了标定的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将概率构建块堆叠成更深层架构通常会破坏闭式推理，本文旨在证明闭式推理可以被保留，从而在深度概率模型中保持计算可处理性。

Method: 识别了五种因子图原语（双线性因子、指数链接、Gamma先验、高斯似然、等式节点），证明在其组成的模型中，通过平均场分解，高斯变量的消息保持高斯分布，精度变量的消息保持Gamma分布，指数链接通过高斯矩生成函数和Gamma充分统计量保持可处理性。从静态集成到输入依赖的门控再到分裂分支路由，展示了不同深度的组合方式，堆叠路由层可编码任意决策树。

Result: 建立了具有闭式推理的通用函数逼近架构；在集成时间序列预测任务中，该方法在五个基准数据集上提供了标定的不确定性估计，门控函数通过推理而非学习获得。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的因子图原语集，可以在深度概率模型中保持闭式变分推理，为贝叶斯深度学习的可计算性提供了新途径。

Abstract: Stacking probabilistic building blocks into deeper architectures typically breaks closed-form inference. We show that closed-form inference can be preserved. We identify five factor-graph primitives: a bilinear factor, an exponential link, a Gamma prior, a Gaussian likelihood, and an equality node, and prove that any model composed from them admits closed-form variational message passing. The construction works because each primitive preserves a small set of message families: under mean-field factorization, messages on Gaussian variables remain Gaussian and messages on precision variables remain Gamma, while the only non-conjugate interface, the exponential link, remains tractable through the Gaussian moment-generating function and the sufficient statistics of the Gamma family. We demonstrate composition at increasing depth, from static ensembles through input-dependent gating to split-branch routing, and show that stacking routing layers encodes arbitrary decision trees, establishing universal function approximation with closed-form inference. Applied to ensemble time-series forecasting, the framework yields a Bayesian mixture of experts in which gating functions are inferred rather than learned, providing calibrated uncertainty over expert selection across five benchmark datasets.

</details>


### [151] [Access Sets Matter: Budgeting Expert Reads for Scalable Weight-Space Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29489)
*Yuanyi Wang,Yanggan Gu,Su Lu,Yifan Yang,Zhaoyi Yan,Congkai Xie,Jianmin Wu,Hongxia Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MergePipe是一种预算感知的模型合并执行层，通过将LLM合并问题转化为专家访问集问题，在显式I/O预算下选择性地读取专家delta块，从而将合并时的专家读取I/O降低一个数量级，并实现最高11倍的加速，同时保持与全读取合并相当的参数精度（偏差约O(10^{-3})）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM规模的权重空间模型合并中，限制性资源通常是必须读取的专家权重集合，而非合并算子本身的代数运算。现有方法未考虑I/O预算对合并效率的影响。

Method: 将LLM合并建模为专家访问集问题，在共享权重坐标系下，给定合并算子和检查点家族，在显式I/O预算约束下选择需访问的专家delta块。MergePipe索引参数块、构建确定性访问计划、执行预算约束合并并生成可回放的清单。对于固定系数加法算子，省略更新的误差由省略delta的范数界定。

Result: 在Qwen和Llama合并任务上，专家读取I/O降低一个数量级，最高实现11倍加速。参数偏差约为O(10^{-3})，下游基准测试无单调性能退化。

Conclusion: MergePipe通过预算感知的专家访问计划，在显著降低I/O开销的同时保持了合并质量，是一种高效且理论上有界的LLM合并方法。

Abstract: Weight-space model merging is usually formulated as an algebraic operation on checkpoints, yet at LLM scale the limiting resource is often the set of expert weights that must be read. We introduce MergePipe, a budget-aware execution layer that casts LLM merging as an \emph{expert access-set} problem: given a merge operator and a checkpoint family in a shared weight coordinate system, choose which expert delta blocks to access under an explicit I/O budget. MergePipe indexes parameter blocks, builds deterministic access plans, and executes the induced budgeted merge with replayable manifests. The plan is budget-sound by construction and recovers the full-read merge at full budget; for fixed-coefficient additive operators, the omitted-update error is bounded by the norm of omitted deltas. Across Qwen and Llama merging workloads, MergePipe reduces expert-read I/O by up to an order of magnitude and achieves up to $11\times$ speedups. Representative budget sweeps show $O(10^{-3})$ parameter deviation from full-read merges and no monotonic degradation on downstream benchmarks.

</details>


### [152] [Gradient Perturbation: Learning to Perturb Gradients for Adaptive Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29494)
*Hua Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统一梯度扰动框架，发现SAM、梯度裁剪、梯度噪声注入等方法均可视为特定形式的梯度扰动。据此提出LPG方法，自适应地在类别级别扰动logit层梯度，实现类别感知训练，在平衡分类、长尾分类和噪声标签学习中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前向传播链（特征扰动、logit扰动、标签扰动）已被广泛研究，但反向传播链中的梯度扰动缺乏系统性研究。本文希望填补这一空白，建立统一的梯度扰动框架。

Method: 1. 建立梯度扰动的统一框架，揭示SAM、梯度裁剪、梯度噪声注入等方法的统一本质。2. 类比Logit Perturbation Learning (LPL)，提出对类别级梯度范数进行放大（正增强）或抑制（负增强）的策略。3. 提出Learning to Perturb Gradients (LPG)，自适应地在类别级别扰动logit层梯度。4. 通过PAC-Bayes分析建立梯度扰动界与泛化保证之间的理论联系。

Result: 在平衡分类、长尾分类和噪声标签学习实验中，LPG一致优于现有方法，且可作为即插即用模块与其他方法结合使用。

Conclusion: 梯度扰动是一个统一的框架，LPG通过类别自适应的梯度扰动实现了类别感知训练，具有良好的泛化性能和兼容性。

Abstract: Deep neural network training involves both forward propagation (from features through logits to loss) and backward propagation (from loss through gradients to parameter updates). While perturbations along the forward chain, including feature perturbation, logit perturbation, and label perturbation, have been extensively studied, the backward chain's gradient perturbation has received little systematic investigation. In this paper, we establish a unified framework for gradient perturbation, revealing that existing methods such as Sharpness-Aware Minimization (SAM), gradient clipping, and gradient noise injection can all be interpreted as imposing specific forms of gradient perturbation. Analogous to the recently proposed Logit Perturbation Learning (LPL), we conjecture that amplifying the gradient norm for a class acts as positive augmentation (enhancing learning), while dampening it acts as negative augmentation (suppressing overfitting). Based on these observations, we propose Learning to Perturb Gradients (LPG), which adaptively perturbs logit-level gradients at the class level to achieve category-aware training. We also establish theoretical connections between gradient perturbation bounds and generalization guarantees via PAC-Bayesian analysis. Experiments on balanced classification, long-tail classification, and noisy label learning demonstrate that LPG consistently outperforms existing methods and can be combined with them as a plug-in module.

</details>


### [153] [On-Policy Replay for Continual Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29495)
*Yan Chen,Taojie Zhu,Meng Zhang,Xin Chen,Jiaqi Huang,Dongyang Xu,Yizhi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为On-Policy Replay (OPR)的持续监督微调方法，通过在历史提示上使用最新模型生成响应并筛选高分样本进行回放，无需额外前向传播、辅助损失或教师模型，在三个7-8B指令微调模型上显著降低了灾难性遗忘，BWT指标提升可达46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的持续监督微调方法在适应下游任务流时存在灾难性遗忘问题。虽然已有研究显示on-policy信号比off-policy监督更能减少遗忘，但现有on-policy方法需要通过新训练目标（如自蒸馏损失）来传递信号，带来了额外前向传播、调度敏感性和教师模型的风格漂移等开销。本文旨在探索一种更简洁的on-policy信号传递方式。

Method: OPR方法的核心思路是将on-policy信号通过训练数据来源传递，而非通过训练目标。具体步骤为：(1) 使用最新模型checkpoint在一小部分历史提示上生成回复；(2) 根据任务奖励对生成结果进行筛选；(3) 将筛选后的(prompt, model response)对作为普通SFT样本进行回放。该方法无需教师模型、辅助损失或在线蒸馏。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct、Qwen3-8B、Llama3.1-8B-Instruct三个模型上，基于TRACE持续学习基准测试，OPR持续降低遗忘。以最严苛的压力测试为例（Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, Sequential SFT BWT -13.93），OPR在10%回放预算下将BWT提升至-0.65，在1%预算下提升至-2.29，相较于调优后的Vanilla Replay基线实现了46%的|BWT|降低，三个模型上均观察到42-46%的降幅。

Conclusion: OPR通过将on-policy信号经由数据源而非训练目标传递，以极简方式有效缓解了持续微调中的灾难性遗忘。本文还给出了KL收缩解释，将OPR与先前的on-policy蒸馏方法统一在同一轴上。此外，一个反直觉的发现是：低分回放始终比Vanilla Replay更差，说明OPR的核心有效成分是on-policy分布，而非单纯的响应质量。

Abstract: Continual supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is the de facto recipe for adapting large language models (LLMs) to a stream of downstream tasks, but it suffers from catastrophic forgetting of earlier capabilities. Recent work shows that on-policy signals -- training on the model's own outputs -- reduce forgetting more reliably than off-policy supervision. Existing on-policy methods route this signal through a new training objective (e.g., self-distillation losses with a teacher copy), inheriting an extra forward pass, schedule sensitivity, and stylistic drift from the teacher.We instead route the on-policy signal through the training data source. Our method, On-Policy Replay (OPR), rolls out the most recent checkpoint on a small budget of historical prompts, filters the generations by a task reward, and replays the surviving (prompt, model response) pairs as ordinary SFT examples. There is no teacher, no auxiliary loss, and no on-the-fly distillation. Across three 7--8B instruction-tuned backbones (Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, Qwen3-8B, Llama3.1-8B-Instruct) on the TRACE continual-learning benchmark, OPR consistently reduces forgetting; on the sharpest stress test (Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, Sequential SFT BWT -13.93), OPR lifts BWT to -0.65 at a 10% replay budget and to -2.29 at a 1% budget -- a 46% reduction in |BWT| over a tuned Vanilla Replay baseline, with 42--46% reductions observed across all three backbones. We give a KL-shrinkage interpretation that places OPR and prior on-policy distillation methods on a single axis, and we present a counterintuitive finding that explains why Vanilla Replay is already a strong baseline: low-score replay is uniformly worse than Vanilla Replay, demonstrating that the active ingredient in OPR is the on-policy distribution, not the response quality alone.Our code is available at https://github.com/Yancey2024/OnPolicyReplay.

</details>


### [154] [Quotient DAGs for Off-Policy Evaluation:Forward-Flow Importance Sampling and Exact Slate Propensities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29500)
*Ziwen Xie,Shaowen Xiang,Hongyu He,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对离线策略评估中行为策略生成过程与评估目标不匹配（如自回归生成有序序列但奖励只依赖无序集合）导致的方差和计算问题，提出基于商DAG的Forward-DP方法，通过子集DAG动态规划精确计算无序倾向性分数，避免了阶乘枚举。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准重要性采样方法在评估时会考虑生成过程的每个细节（如推荐顺序），但评估目标（如奖励函数）可能只依赖无序结果，这引入了不必要的方差，且计算精确的无序倾向性需要枚举所有可能的生成顺序，计算成本高昂。

Method: 提出商DAG视角——合并对评估等价的轨迹历史，在合并后的图上使用目标策略到行为策略的前向流比率来分配权重。针对集合充分的下一个物品接口的场景，进一步提出Forward-DP，一种子集DAG动态规划算法，无需阶乘枚举即可精确计算无序倾向性分数。

Result: Forward-DP能够精确计算无序倾向性分数，避免了阶乘枚举的计算开销，为基于倾向性的评估和模型选择提供了实用的原始工具。

Conclusion: 所提方法适用于依赖上下文的自回归高维推荐日志器的离线评估和模型选择，解决了生成顺序与评估目标不匹配导致的统计和计算问题。

Abstract: Off-policy evaluation estimates how a target policy would perform using data collected by a different behavior policy, which is crucial when online testing is costly or risky, such as in recommendation or healthcare. Standard importance sampling reweights each logged trajectory, but it can treat details of the generation process as meaningful even when the evaluation target ignores them: for example, an autoregressive slate recommender may generate an ordered sequence of items while the reward and downstream estimator depend only on the unordered slate. This creates nuisance variance and a computational gap, since exact unordered slate propensities require summing over all generation orders. We introduce a quotient-DAG view that merges histories equivalent for evaluation and assigns weights using target-to-behavior forward-flow ratios on the merged graph. For slate recommendation under a set-sufficient next-item interface, this yields Forward-DP, a subset-DAG dynamic program that computes exact unordered propensities without factorial enumeration. The resulting propensity primitive enables practical propensity-based evaluation and model selection for context-dependent autoregressive slate loggers.

</details>


### [155] [K-FinHallu: A Hallucination Detection Benchmark for Multi-Turn RAG in Korean Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29523)
*Eunbyeol Cho,Yunseung Lee,Mirae Kim,Jeewon Yang,Youngjun Kwak,Edward Choi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了K-FinHallu，首个面向韩语金融领域多轮RAG幻觉检测的基准，发现现有模型在细粒度金融诊断和合理拒答方面表现薄弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测基准集中于单轮、英语场景，缺乏对韩语金融领域多轮对话及语言-监管细微差异的覆盖，阻碍了LLM在金融自动化中的实际部署。

Method: 从真实韩语金融文档构建多轮对话，基于上下文可回答性提出分层幻觉注入分类体系（含合理拒答），将前沿及开源LLM作为幻觉检测器进行评测，并对8B模型进行微调。

Result: 最强模型在细粒度金融诊断和拒答行为上仍表现困难；微调后的8B模型性能接近前沿LLM，但合理拒答在所有评估模型中均为最薄弱维度。

Conclusion: 韩语金融多轮RAG场景中幻觉检测极具挑战性，合理拒答能力是当前模型的共同瓶颈，亟需针对性改进。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have advanced financial automation through Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), yet hallucinations remain a critical barrier to deployment in high-stakes environments. Existing benchmarks focus on single-turn, English-centric tasks, leaving the multi-turn dynamics and linguistic-regulatory nuances of the Korean financial domain unaddressed. We introduce K-FinHallu, the first benchmark for hallucination detection in multi-turn Korean financial RAG. We construct multi-turn dialogues from authentic Korean financial documents and inject hallucinations under a proposed hierarchical taxonomy based on context answerability that explicitly accounts for justified abstention. Benchmarking frontier and open-source LLMs as hallucination detectors, we find that even the strongest models struggle with fine-grained financial diagnostics and refusal behavior. While fine-tuning an 8B model on our training split yields performance competitive with frontier LLMs, justified abstention remains the weakest axis across all evaluated models.

</details>


### [156] [AsymVLM: Asymmetric Token Pruning for Efficient Vision-Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29535)
*Yilin Feng,Ahmed Burak Gulhan,Mahmut Taylan Kandemir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出非对称压缩方法AsymVLM，对视觉token进行激进剪枝（基于学习的重要性评分器+自适应预算），对文本token进行基于阈值的驱逐，在节省高达54% FLOPs的同时，在文档/图表理解任务上超越现有方法2-3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM压缩方法对视觉和文本token采用统一处理，忽略了两种模态的根本差异：视觉token空间冗余且主导预填充，文本token因果依赖且在解码阶段累积。

Method: 提出AsymVLM，包含两个非对称策略：(1) 预填充前使用学习的重要性评分器对视觉token进行激进剪枝，并采用每个样本自适应预算；(2) 对文本token采用基于时间阈值的驱逐机制，仅在超出固定预算时触发。

Result: 在state-of-the-art方法中实现了最高的FLOPs节省（高达54%）；在文档和图表理解任务上超越现有方法2-3%；在文本主导场景中，驱逐策略显著优于标准LLM缓存压缩方法。

Conclusion: 利用视觉与文本token的固有不对称性设计差异化的压缩策略，能够在保持整体准确率的同时实现更高效的计算节省。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) process thousands of visual tokens per image alongside comparatively few text tokens, yet existing compression methods treat both modalities uniformly. We observe that the two modalities have fundamentally different properties: vision tokens are spatially redundant and dominate prefill, while text tokens are causally dependent and accumulate during decoding. Based on this asymmetry, we propose and empirically evaluate AsymVLM, which applies aggressive pruning to vision tokens before prefill using a learned importance scorer with per-sample adaptive budgeting, and temporal threshold-based eviction to text tokens only when they exceed a fixed budget. Our experiments indicate that AsymVLM achieves the highest FLOPs savings (up to 54%) among state-of-the-art methods while outperforming existing approaches by 2--3% on document and chart understanding tasks where visual information is spatially localized and query-specific, and maintaining competitive accuracy on holistic benchmarks. In text-dominated scenarios, our eviction strategy substantially outperforms standard LLM cache compression methods by adapting to the short-context nature of VLM.

</details>


### [157] [SCOPE: A Lightweight-training LLM Framework for Air Traffic Control Readback Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29543)
*Qihan Deng,Minghua Zhang,Yang Yang,Zhenyu Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SCOPE框架，一种轻量级训练的LLM方法，通过耦合开放集分类器和上下文学习机制，在冻结LLM基础上实现高效准确的ATC复诵监控，在少样本条件下达到91.05%的开放集检测准确率和96.63%的异常复诵纠正率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ATC复诵异常涉及约80%的航空事故，传统规则和机器学习方法难以适应高度可变且不断演变的陆空通话用语，现有LLM方法存在部署和计算障碍，亟需一种高效、准确且可部署的自动化复诵监控方案。

Method: 提出SCOPE框架，核心是将开放集插件分类器与精心设计的上下文学习机制相结合，在冻结的LLM之上运行，仅需轻量级训练即可实现语义推理。该方法通过开放集检测识别异常复诵，并利用LLM的推理能力提供可解释的决策。

Result: 在半合成通信数据集上的实验表明，SCOPE在少样本设定下达到91.05%的开放集检测准确率，纠正96.63%的异常复诵，优于最强基线方法，同时满足运行环境的低延迟要求。

Conclusion: SCOPE框架展示了作为可解释、可控的ATC复诵监控实际路径的潜力，为航空安全中的自动化陆空通话检测提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Pilot readback of Air Traffic Control (ATC) voice instructions is a primary safeguard against miscommunication in air transportation. However, readback anomalies remain implicated in approximately 80% of aviation incidents. This vulnerability is further exacerbated by rising traffic volume and elevated cognitive workload, thereby motivating automated readback monitoring by machine. Traditional rule-based and machine learning approaches struggle to generalize across the highly variable and evolving phraseology of air traffic controller-pilot communications. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have opened a new avenue through their strong reasoning and generalization capabilities, existing approaches still face deployment and computational barriers in practice. In this work, we propose Semantic reasoning for Communication via Open-set Plug-in with Examples (SCOPE), a novel lightweight-training LLM framework that advances both the efficiency and accuracy of machine-based ATC readback monitoring. The core idea is to couple a plug-in open-set classifier with a carefully designed in-context learning mechanism on top of a frozen LLM. Extensive experiments on the semi-synthetic communication dataset show that SCOPE attains superior accuracy while delivering the low-latency response required for operational environments. Under a few-shot setting, SCOPE achieves 91.05% accuracy in open-set detection and corrects 96.63% of anomalous readbacks, thereby outperforming the strongest available baselines while providing explanations for its decisions. These findings demonstrate the potential of our framework as a practical pathway toward interpretable and controllable ATC readback monitoring.

</details>


### [158] [Singularity-aware Optimization via Randomized Geometric Probing: Towards Stable Non-smooth Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29547)
*Ruoran Xu,Borong She,Xiaobo Jin,Qiufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出S-Adam优化器，通过局部几何不稳定性（LGI）度量动态调整学习率，解决非平滑损失景观下Adam优化器的梯度抖动问题，在QAT和高噪声小批量学习中提升准确率，并证明了几乎必然收敛到(δ,ε)-Clarke驻点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习架构（如ReLU激活、量化算子）引入非平滑组件，导致损失景观不光滑，传统Adam优化器因Clarke次微分中的冲突信号而产生梯度抖动，收敛性能差且泛化能力不佳。

Method: 提出S-Adam，核心包括：(1) LGI度量，通过随机方向导数的方差高效估计Clarke次微分直径；(2) 自适应阻尼机制 exp(-λρ)，在高不稳定性区域降低更新步长，在平滑区域保持快速收敛。

Result: 在量化感知训练和高噪声小批量学习任务上，S-Adam持续优于AdamW和Prox-SGD，在CIFAR-100上准确率提升达6%，在TinyImageNet上提升达3%，有效缓解了梯度振荡。

Conclusion: S-Adam通过几何感知的步长调节机制，在非平滑优化场景下实现了更优的收敛性和泛化性能，且具有理论保证（几乎必然收敛到(δ,ε)-Clarke驻点，最优O(1/√T)速率）。

Abstract: Deep learning optimization relies heavily on the assumption of smooth loss landscapes, a condition systematically violated by modern architectures due to non-smooth components such as ReLU activations and quantization operators. In such non-smooth regimes, adaptive optimizers such as Adam suffer from gradient chattering, violent oscillations caused by conflicting signals within the Clarke subdifferential, leading to poor convergence and suboptimal generalization. To address this, we introduce Singularity-aware Adam (S-Adam), a novel optimizer that stabilizes training by dynamically modulating step sizes based on local geometric instability. Our key contribution is the Local Geometric Instability (LGI) metric, a computationally efficient estimator of the Clarke subdifferential diameter derived from the variance of randomized directional derivatives. S-Adam incorporates an adaptive damping mechanism exp(-$λ$$ρ$) that decelerates updates in high-instability regions while preserving fast convergence in smooth basins. We provide a rigorous convergence analysis using differential inclusions, proving that S-Adam converges almost surely to ($δ$,$ε$)-Clarke stationary points at the optimal O(1/$\sqrt(T)$) rate. Empirical evaluations on Quantization-Aware Training (QAT) and high-noise small-batch learning demonstrate that S-Adam consistently outperforms AdamW and Prox-SGD, achieving accuracy gains of up to 6 percent on CIFAR-100 and 3 percent on TinyImageNet while effectively mitigating gradient oscillations.

</details>


### [159] [Why Larger Models Learn More: Effects of Capacity, Interference, and Rare-Task Retention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29548)
*Jing Huang,Daniel Wurgaft,Rachit Bansal,Laura Ruis,Naomi Saphra,David Alvarez-Melis,Andrew Kyle Lampinen,Christopher Potts,Ekdeep Singh Lubana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过理论论证和实验验证，解释了为什么更大模型能学习更小模型无法学习的任务：大模型能够克服数据驱动的资源竞争瓶颈，减少任务间的梯度干扰，从而学习到低频和复杂任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示更大模型能够学习小模型无法学习任务的深层原因，提供数据中心的解释，并为模型规模设计和训练数据配比提供实践指导。

Method: 首先基于幂律缩放提出现象学论证；然后在合成混合任务数据集上研究模型缩放效应，分析神经元资源分配和梯度干扰机制；最后在OLMo模型（4M到4B参数）上预训练不同频率和复杂度的新任务进行验证。

Result: 小模型将神经元优先分配给高频或低复杂度任务，导致在罕见和复杂任务上表现差；大模型通过减少任务间梯度干扰（为常见任务分配足够资源使其梯度更新变弱，不会覆盖罕见任务特征）来克服这一瓶颈。OLMo实验与合成实验一致：仅大模型能学习低频复杂任务，且大模型在表征中嵌入更多任务特征、任务间梯度干扰更小。

Conclusion: 提供了数据中心的解释说明为何更大模型能学习小模型无法学习的任务，有助于理解大模型在实际中表现更优的原因，并为模型规模设计和训练数据配比提供参考。

Abstract: Larger models learn tasks smaller models do not. What drives this phenomenon? We develop a simple phenomenological argument that power-law scaling already suggests that a larger model will be able to learn a part of the data distribution that a smaller model fails to learn, even with infinite training data. To validate this claim and identify its causes, we study the effects of model scaling on a synthetic setup consisting of a mixture of tasks that show monotonic scaling curves. The results point to a data-induced competition over resources (neurons). Specifically, smaller models allocate their neurons to high frequency or low complexity tasks, and so they learn solutions that perform poorly on rare and complex tasks. Moreover, this happens even when solutions capable of expressing the desired task exist. We then assess how a larger model circumvents this data-centric bottleneck, finding that it traces to a reduced interference mechanism: larger models can allocate enough resources to common tasks that the gradient updates for those tasks become weak, which means that they do not overwrite rare-task features as they slowly accumulate. Finally, to further validate these claims, we pretrain OLMo models (4M to 4B parameters) on novel tasks of varying frequency and complexity. The results mirror those from our synthetic data experiments: only the larger OLMo models learn the infrequent and complex tasks, and these larger models embed more task features in their representations and show less gradient interference between tasks. Overall, we offer a data-centric account of why larger models learn tasks that smaller models fail to. This helps explain why larger models are better in practice, and it can inform practical questions concerning model sizing and training data mixtures.

</details>


### [160] [On the Construction and Implications of Low-Loss Valleys in LoRA-based Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29580)
*Daniel Dold,Emanuel Sommer,Julius Kobialka,Oliver Dürr,David Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoRA-Curve，一种在LoRA参数空间中用分段贝塞尔曲线连接多个最优解的方法，并通过连续低损失谷实现功能多样性，在不牺牲性能的前提下提升预测分布的互信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA微调中，离散多模型集成（如深度集成）相比单模型方法收益有限，这与深度学习中对集成能提升泛化能力的普遍认知相矛盾。是否在LoRA空间中存在连续低损失谷结构并将多个最优解连接起来，从而带来局部或离散方法所缺失的功能多样性，这一问题尚未被研究。

Method: 提出LoRA-Curve方法：在LoRA参数空间中使用分段贝塞尔曲线进行参数化，包含两种变体——自由配置（联合优化所有控制点）和锚定配置（连接独立微调得到的LoRA最优解）。理论证明了沿曲线的路径连续性和Lipschitz正则性。结合平坦极小值扰动和Jensen-Shannon散度正则化项进行优化。

Result: 在Qwen2.5 7B模型上的推理和分类基准实验中，线性插值存在损失屏障，而锚定多段曲线能够通过连续低损失谷连接独立最优解。LoRA-Curve在不牺牲性能的前提下，显著提升了预测分布的互信息，建立了参数空间连续遍历与功能多样性之间的联系。

Conclusion: LoRA空间中存在连续低损失谷结构，通过分段贝塞尔曲线可以连接多个独立LoRA最优解，从而获得离散集成方法难以实现的功能多样性。该发现挑战了LoRA场景下集成方法无益的已有结论。

Abstract: While parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods like low-rank adaptation (LoRA) are standard for large language models, principled estimation of epistemic uncertainty remains challenging. Recent results in the LoRA regime suggest that discrete multi-mode approaches such as deep ensembles offer little benefit over single-mode methods. This contradicts broader observations in deep learning, where ensembling independent optima typically improves generalization, and linking these modes through continuous low-loss valleys further enhances Bayesian model averaging (BMA). Whether such structure exists in the LoRA space and whether it yields functional diversity missed by local or discrete methods has not been studied. We introduce LoRA-Curve, a segmented Bézier curve parameterization in the LoRA space, with two variants: a free configuration that jointly optimizes all control points, and an anchored configuration that connects independently fine-tuned LoRA optima. We prove pathwise continuity and Lipschitz regularity of the loss along the curve and empirically show, across reasoning and classification benchmarks with Qwen2.5 7B, that linear interpolation encounters loss barriers, while our anchored multi-segment curves connect independent optima through continuous low-loss valleys. Combined with flat-minima perturbations and a Jensen-Shannon divergence regularizer, LoRA-Curve yields measurably higher mutual information of the predictive distribution without sacrificing performance, and links continuous parameter-space traversal to functional diversity.

</details>


### [161] [Cluster-Level Attention-Guided Parallel Decoding for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29607)
*Heqiang Qi,Wei Huang,Mingyuan Bai,Xiangming Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CLAD方法，将掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）的并行解码粒度从token级提升到span级。通过置信度诱导聚类（CIC）将相邻高置信度token聚合成span级更新单元，并利用自注意力图估计聚类间依赖关系，实现冲突感知的并行选择。在LLaDA和Dream模型上，CLAD在推理和代码生成任务中实现1.77倍至8.47倍加速，同时保持大体相当的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MDLM的训练无关采样器以token级粒度决定哪些位置进行提交，但观察到可靠预测往往以连续高置信度span的形式出现，说明并行提交的基本单元可以大于单个token，因此需要设计更高效的跨度级解码策略。

Method: 1) 将相邻高置信度token聚合成置信度诱导聚类（CIC）作为span级更新单元；2) 利用同一前向传播中的自注意力图估计聚类间依赖关系；3) 基于依赖关系进行冲突感知的聚类选择，实现并行提交。

Result: 在LLaDA和Dream模型家族的四个推理和代码生成基准上，CLAD实现1.77倍至8.47倍的加速，且在大多数设置下任务准确率与基线基本持平。

Conclusion: 将MDLM解码粒度从token级提升到聚类级是可行且高效的，CLAD方法无需训练即可显著加速推理，为掩码扩散语言模型的高效解码提供了新思路。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) enable parallel decoding by predicting all masked positions at each denoising step, yet existing training-free samplers usually decide which positions to commit at token-level granularity. We revisit this granularity and observe that reliable predictions often emerge as contiguous high-confidence spans, suggesting that the unit of parallel commitment can be larger than a single token. We first group adjacent high-confidence candidates into confidence-induced clusters (CICs) as span-level update units. We then use self-attention maps from the same forward pass to estimate inter-cluster dependencies, enabling conflict-aware selection of mutually compatible CICs for parallel commitment. This yields CLAD (Cluster-Level Attention-Guided Decoding), a training-free cluster-level decoder for MDLMs. Experiments on LLaDA and Dream model families across four reasoning and code-generation benchmarks show that CLAD achieves 1.77x--8.47x speedups over Vanilla decoding while maintaining broadly comparable task accuracy in most settings.

</details>


### [162] [MōLe-Λ: Learning the Coupled-Cluster Response State for Energies, Gradients, and Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29622)
*Andreas Burger,Luca Thiede,Abdulrahman Aldossary,Jorge A. Campos-Gonzalez-Angulo,Alex Zook,Jérôme Florian Gonthier,Alán Aspuru-Guzik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MōLe-Λ 通过联合学习 CCSD 的左、右手振幅（T 和 Λ），在保持 MōLe 的精度和效率优势的同时，扩展了可预测的分子性质范围，包括偶极矩、四极矩、极化率、电子密度和双电子密度等可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CCSD 的高计算成本限制了其广泛应用，且许多重要可观测量（如偶极矩、极化率等）需要计算左手的 Λ 振幅，而现有 MōLe 仅预测右手 T 振幅，无法直接获取这些性质。

Method: 在 MōLe 的等变轨道编码器和奇宇称符号等变解码器基础上，新增 Λ₁ 和 Λ₂ 读出头，与 T₁、T₂ 头保持相同的对称性约束，联合学习 (T₁,T₂) 和 (Λ₁,Λ₂)，保持局域性、大小广延性和计算效率。

Result: MōLe-Λ 能准确预测 CCSD 级别的能量和力，同时恢复偶极矩、四极矩、极化率、电子密度以及双电子密度（对密度）等可观测量，并保持了 MōLe 相比完整 CCSD 的速度优势。

Conclusion: MōLe-Λ 为关联量子化学提供了波函数级别的替代模型，显著扩展了可访问的分子性质，是在精度和效率之间实现平衡的有效方法。

Abstract: Coupled-cluster (CC) theory is often considered the gold standard of quantum chemistry, but its high computational cost limits routine access to accurate energies, forces and response properties. While the right-hand $T$-amplitudes determine the correlated wavefunction, many practically important observables additionally require the left-hand $Λ$-amplitudes. We introduce MōLe-$Λ$, an extension of Molecular Orbital Learning (MōLe) that predicts the full ground-state coupled-cluster singles and doubles (CCSD) response state by jointly learning right-hand amplitudes $(T_1,T_2)$ and left-hand amplitudes $(Λ_1,Λ_2)$ from localized Hartree--Fock molecular orbitals. Architecturally, MōLe-$Λ$ extends MōLe with $Λ_1$ and $Λ_2$ readouts that mirror the symmetry constraints of the $T_1$ and $T_2$ heads, while preserving the original equivariant orbital encoder, odd sign-equivariant decoding, locality and size-extensivity. The resulting model yields accurate CC-quality energies and forces, while simultaneously recovering dipoles, quadrupoles, polarizabilities, the electron density, and 2-electron observables such as the pair density. We show that MōLe-$Λ$ further extends the speed advantage of MōLe over full CCSD while substantially expanding the accessible properties, providing a route to wavefunction-level surrogate models for correlated quantum chemistry.

</details>


### [163] [Relational Rank Geometry in Transformers: Detecting and Steering Hidden-State Relation Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29634)
*Mazen Kobrosly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Transformer隐空间中元组关系的秩索引几何结构（rank-indexed geometry），通过Plücker符号熵检测r元关系在隐状态空间中是否留下与元数匹配的定向特征，并在Llama家族模型（8B/70B/405B）上验证了这种几何结构的可检测性、鲁棒性及可干预性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer隐状态解释多聚焦于局部或低阶对象（神经元、稀疏特征、注意力头等），缺乏对高阶元组关系（r-argument relations）的几何结构理解。本文旨在填补这一空白，探索关系元数与隐空间定向几何之间的对应关系。

Method: 1) 使用Plücker符号熵检测r元关系是否在隐状态空间中留下与元数r匹配的定向签名（r=3,...,6）；2) 通过多模板审计验证表面变体下的鲁棒性；3) 在边格干净/污染干预实验中，通过将污染隐状态的关系框架修补至干净/安慰剂目标，检验关系几何的可操控性，并设置质心控制、等范数控制、位点/顺序控制等对照。

Result: 1) 所有Llama系列检查点上，真实关系元组在预期秩k=r处表现出比打乱元组更强的定向符号一致性；2) 多模板审计中405B模型所有行均保持正期望秩边际，8B/70B部分行保持正结果；3) 在70B和405B上，干净目标的关系框架路径成功恢复干净答案行为和残差关系几何，而质心控制、等范数控制等对照方法恢复效果可忽略；4) 位点/顺序控制实验表明目标干净形状和跨提示干净形状在标记接口处能恢复行为与残差几何，而污染供体转移等对照失败。

Conclusion: 本文建立了从关系探测到关系框架干预的可控桥梁：Transformer隐状态中的关系秩几何可被检测、可被靶向、并可通过行为实验验证，为理解和操控高阶关系提供了新的几何视角。

Abstract: Transformer hidden states are often interpreted through local or low-order objects: neurons, sparse features, attention heads, residual-stream directions, or activation patches. This paper studies a complementary object: the rank-indexed geometry of relations among token tuples. I use Plucker sign entropy to test whether r-argument relations leave arity-matched orientation signatures in hidden-state space. Across Llama-family 8B, 70B, and 405B checkpoints, true relation tuples show stronger orientation-sign consistency at the expected rank k=r for r=3,...,6 than scrambled tuples under matched random-control audits. Multi-template audits show that the effects survive surface variation, with all tested 405B rows retaining positive expected-rank margins and 8B/70B retaining positive rows with constructor-specific mixed cells. I then ask whether the same relation geometry can be steered. In an edge-grid clean/corrupt intervention assay over 32 prompts, the row/column scaffold and answer format stay fixed while the YES/NO relation map changes, and the corrupt hidden-state relation frame is patched toward clean or placebo targets. In 70B and 405B, clean-targeted relation-frame paths recover clean-answer behavior and residual relation geometry, while centroid-only and equal-norm controls show negligible recovery. Site/order controls further separate marker-site importance from ordered clean-frame geometry: target clean shape and cross-prompt clean shape recover behavior and residual geometry at the marker interface, whereas corrupt-donor transfer, same-site permutation/reflection, wrong-site clean deltas, centroid-only motion, and equal-norm noise fail or remain far below clean-frame paths. The result is a controlled bridge from relation probing to relation-frame intervention: relation rank geometry can be detected, targeted, and behaviorally validated in transformer hidden states.

</details>


### [164] [The Sample Complexity of Multiclass and Sparse Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29645)
*Liad Erez,Fan Chen,Alon Cohen,Tomer Koren,Yishay Mansour,Shay Moran,Alexander Rakhlin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究随机上下文赌博机中s-稀疏奖励设置下的最优策略学习，提出了两种互补算法（基于决策估计系数的信息论方法和基于低方差探索的实用方法），实现了与下界匹配的样本复杂度 $\tilde{O}((s/\epsilon^2 + |A|/\epsilon)\log(|\Pi|/\delta))$，显著改进了先前工作中 $O(|A|^9)$ 的额外依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的上下文赌博机算法在处理稀疏奖励（如 bandit 多类分类中零一奖励）时，样本复杂度存在 $\Theta(|A|^9)$ 的额外项，这是一个巨大的理论间隙。本文旨在消除这一间隙，获得与动作空间大小 $|A|$ 和稀疏度 $s$ 匹配的最优样本复杂度。

Method: 两种互补方法：(1) 基于决策估计系数（DEC）的探索-优化算法，通过分析 s-稀疏奖励下模型类的 DEC 界，从信息论角度推导最优率；(2) 基于低方差探索的实用算法，产生具体可解的算法并自然扩展到上下文组合半赌博机设置（如 bandit 多类列表分类）。

Result: 实现了 $\tilde{O}((s/\epsilon^2 + |A|/\epsilon)\log(|\Pi|/\delta))$ 的样本复杂度，该界可推广到一般 Natarajan 类，并搭配了匹配的下界（对数因子内），彻底闭合了先前工作的间隙。

Conclusion: 本文解决了稀疏奖励上下文赌博机中样本复杂度的关键理论间隙，提供了从信息论到实用算法的完整解决方案，并自然扩展到组合半赌博机等更广泛的设置。

Abstract: We study contextual bandits in the stochastic i.i.d.\ setting, where a learner observes contexts drawn from an unknown distribution, selects actions from a finite set $A$, and aims to identify an approximately optimal policy from a given class based on bandit feedback. Motivated by bandit multiclass classification with zero-one rewards, we focus on the \emph{$s$-sparse} setting in which, for every context, the reward vector has $L_1$-norm at most $s \ll |A|$. Our main result is the design of algorithms that, with high probability, output an $ε$-optimal policy compared to policy class $Π$ using $\tilde{O} ((s/ε^2 + |A|/ε)\log |Π|/δ)$ samples. We extend this bound to general Natarajan classes and complement it with a matching lower bound (up to logarithmic factors), thereby closing a substantial gap left by prior work (Erez et al., 2024, 2025), which incurred an additional $Θ(|A|^9)$ dependence. We obtain these results via two complementary approaches. First, we analyze contextual bandits through the lens of contextual decision making with structured observations, designing an exploration-by-optimization algorithm whose sample complexity is governed by the \emph{decision-estimation coefficient} (DEC; Foster et al., 2021, 2022). We show that, with $s$-sparse rewards, the induced model class admits a sharp DEC bound that scales with $s$ and directly yields the optimal rate. Since this approach is largely information-theoretic and involves solving complex min-max optimization problems, we also develop a second, more specialized algorithmic method based on a low-variance exploration technique. This approach leads to concrete, tractable algorithms and naturally extends to contextual combinatorial semi-bandits, leading to improved sample complexity guarantees for bandit multiclass list classification.

</details>


### [165] [Opir: Efficient Multi-Task Safety Classification for Toxicity, Jailbreaks, Hate Speech, and Harmful Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29659)
*Ihor Stepanov,Aleksandr Smechov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Opir，一个基于GLiClass架构的编码器型护栏模型系列，涵盖多任务安全分类、毒性检测、越狱检测及零样本不安全内容分类，并发布边缘设备变体。模型在包含996个类别的三层分类体系上训练，结合多种数据来源。在12项安全分类和17项类别任务上，Opir以更小的部署规模在多数基准上达到或超越现有最强开源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型应用需要实时安全过滤，但现有方案存在两难：大型护栏模型成本高，而小型模型难以区分良性敏感文本与真正的有害内容。Opir旨在提供兼具高精度与低部署成本的安全分类器。

Method: 基于GLiClass架构构建编码器型护栏模型，训练数据融合三层分类体系（996类别）、对抗挖掘难例、良性安全样本、多语言翻译及Aegis2/WildGuard子集；支持多任务（二元安全分类、多标签毒性、越狱检测、零样本分类）；发布<100M参数的边缘变体；开源评估框架支持多种后端。

Result: 在12项安全分类和17项类别任务上，Opir与8个当代护栏系统（包括GLiNER2和生成式模型）对比，在多数基准上达到或超越最强开源基线，且部署规模显著更小。

Conclusion: Opir通过编码器架构和精细分类体系实现了高精度安全过滤与低部署成本的有效权衡，为LLM实时安全防护提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-time safety filtering for large language model (LLM) applications requires classifiers that can detect unsafe prompts, toxic language, jailbreak attempts, and unsafe responses without the cost profile of large guardrail models, and that can distinguish benign sensitive text from genuinely covert harmful content. In this paper, we introduce Opir, a family of encoder-based guardrail models built on the GLiClass architecture. Opir includes multi-task models for binary safe/unsafe classification, multi-label toxicity classification, jailbreak classification, and zero-shot unsafe prompt and response categorization. We also release edge variants with fewer than 100M parameters dedicated to binary safe/unsafe categorization. The models are trained on a three-level taxonomy containing 996 categories across 16 top-level labels, 126 mid-level labels, and 854 leaf labels. Opir's training data combines taxonomy-grounded unsafe prompts, adversarially mined hard negatives, benign safety-preserving examples, generated response examples, multilingual translations, and portions of the Aegis2 and WildGuard training subsets. We also open-sourced an evaluation harness that supports GLiClass and GLiNER2 backends as well as decoder-based models, and covers binary safety classification, multi-label categorization, toxicity, jailbreak detection, prompt safety, response safety, response refusal, and prompt subcategory views across public benchmark families. Across an expanded comparison spanning 12 safety-classification tasks and 17 category tasks against eight contemporary guardrail systems -- including both GLiNER2-based and generative guardrail models -- Opir variants are competitive on or ahead of the strongest open-weight baselines on the majority of benchmark datasets while operating with a substantially smaller deployment footprint.

</details>


### [166] [A Geometric View of SRC: Learning Representations for Stable Residual Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29673)
*Vangelis P. Oikonomou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对基于重构的推理方法（如SRC），提出了一种几何导向的训练目标，在不引入SRC残差或预测参与训练的情况下，通过优化类条件子空间几何性质（减小子空间重叠、主导性和近重叠）来提升残差排序稳定性，并在COIL-100、TREC和EEG数据集上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于重构的推理（如SRC）的可靠性依赖于学习到的表示的几何结构，但现有方法常将SRC参与训练（微分、展开或优化），导致训练与推理耦合。本文希望保持严格的训练-推理分离，仅通过优化表示几何来提升SRC在测试时的推理性能。

Method: 1. 提出跨度级理想化框架，形式化残差排序稳定性（残差间隔）及几何障碍（跨度重叠、主导性、通过小主角导致的近重叠）。2. 推导理想化残差间隔的下界（覆盖与分离假设下）。3. 提出几何导向训练目标：促进掩码类内自表达、抑制跨类重构路径和跨类跨度对齐、防止坍塌。4. 在图像、文本、EEG三种模态下使用固定SRC/OMP推理进行评估。

Result: 在COIL-100（图像）、TREC（文本）和EEG连接数据上，使用固定SRC/OMP推理，本文方法在残差间隔和几何诊断指标上优于/可比于交叉熵参考几何，验证了几何导向训练的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过显式优化表示空间的类条件子空间几何性质，可以在严格训练-推理分离的前提下提升基于重构的推理方法的性能，为理解SRC等方法的可靠性和设计更好的表示学习目标提供了理论基础与实践指南。

Abstract: Reconstruction-based inference assigns a class by comparing class-wise reconstruction residuals; Sparse Representation Classification (SRC) is a canonical instance whose reliability depends on the geometry of the learned representation. We adopt a strict training-inference separation: SRC is used only as a fixed test-time rule and is never differentiated, unrolled, or optimized during training. In a span-level idealization based on class-conditional spans and their associated projection residuals, we formalize residual-ordering stability through a residual margin and characterize geometric obstructions -- span overlap, dominance, and near-overlap via small principal angles -- that can collapse this margin in worst-case directions. This span-level theory is primary: it specifies when the idealized residual family is well-separated, and it provides a conditional solver-level interpretation for practical residual approximations (e.g., OMP) insofar as they remain close to the span-level residual ordering. Under explicit coverage and separation assumptions, we derive a quantitative lower bound on the (idealized) residual margin. Guided by these targets, we propose geometry-shaping objectives that promote masked within-class self-expressiveness, discourage cross-class reconstruction pathways and inter-class span alignment, and prevent collapse -- without invoking SRC residuals or predictions during training. Experiments on images (COIL-100), text (TREC), and EEG connectivity evaluate all representations under identical fixed SRC/OMP inference and report residual margins and geometric diagnostics; cross-entropy is included only as a reference geometry under the same evaluation protocol.

</details>


### [167] [Momentum Based Reward Design for Low Emission Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29693)
*Chinmay Mundane,Amith Manoharan,Arun Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于动量的奖励函数（MBRF），用于深度强化学习（DRL）的交通信号控制，通过在SUMO模拟中进行评估，该方法相比传统基于延迟或队列长度的奖励以及经典控制器（如Max Pressure和LQF），在吞吐量、排放和训练稳定性方面表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的交通信号控制系统难以适应动态交通状况，而现有的基于延迟和队列长度的DRL奖励函数往往产生短视或不稳定的策略，需要更好的奖励设计来提升自适应交通信号控制的性能。

Method: 提出动量奖励函数（MBRF），核心思想是鼓励车辆持续移动而非仅仅惩罚拥堵，该奖励函数集成到DRL框架中，并在SUMO仿真平台上进行评估。

Result: 该奖励函数在等待时间、队列长度、吞吐量和CO2排放等标准交通指标上，相比基于延迟或队列长度的奖励以及Max Pressure和LQF等经典控制器，取得了更好的吞吐量-排放权衡和更稳定的学习行为。

Conclusion: 基于动量的奖励函数能有效提升DRL在自适应交通信号控制中的性能，改善吞吐量与排放之间的权衡，并提高训练稳定性。

Abstract: Urban traffic congestion is a growing global issue contributing significantly to long commute times and environmental pollution. Traditional traffic signal control systems often fail to adapt to dynamic traffic conditions. Adaptive traffic signal control can improve urban traffic without changing road infrastructure. Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) has shown strong performance for this task, but existing delay and queue-based rewards often produce short-sighted or unstable policies. This paper proposes a Momentum-Based Reward Function (MBRF) that encourages vehicles to keep moving rather than penalizing congestion alone. The method is evaluated in SUMO (Simulation of Urban MObility) using standard traffic metrics such as waiting time, queue length, throughput, and CO2 emissions. Results show that the proposed reward produces better throughput-emission trade-offs and more stable learning behavior than delay or queue-based rewards, as well as classical controllers such as Max Pressure and LQF.

</details>


### [168] [A Systematic Evaluation of Molecular Mixture Behavior Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29698)
*Roel J. Leenhouts,Nathan K. Morgan,William Green,Jan G. Rittig,Florence H. Vermeire*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有分子性质预测机器学习主要针对纯化合物，但实际应用涉及混合物。作者提出一种分解混合物性质误差的评估框架，将误差拆分为纯组分贡献和非理想相互作用部分。通过匹配的纯物质与混合物数据集实验发现，高绝对精度可能掩盖非理想混合行为的低预测能力，且严格分子划分下性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前分子性质预测的评估仅关注绝对精度，但混合物误差中混杂了纯组分贡献与理想混合偏差，无法真实反映模型对分子间相互作用的学习能力。

Method: 提出误差分解评估框架，包含：(1) 泄露感知的数据划分策略；(2) 理想混合物基线模型；(3) 过量性质度量指标。同时整理了7个匹配的纯物质-混合物物理化学性质数据集。

Result: 多种混合物性质任务和模型族上的实验表明：高绝对精度可能掩盖对非理想混合行为的差恢复能力；在严格分子划分下性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 将性质预测泛化到未见分子是混合物机器学习的关键挑战，评估不能仅依赖绝对精度。

Abstract: Machine learning for molecular property prediction has focused largely on pure compounds, even though many practical applications depend on mixtures with intermolecular interactions. Recent work has expanded the availability of mixture datasets, but evaluation still focuses mainly on absolute accuracy. However, absolute errors in mixtures conflate pure-component contributions with deviations from ideal mixing. We propose an evaluation framework that decomposes mixture-property error into pure-compound and interaction (non-ideal) components. The framework combines leakage-aware split protocols, ideal-mixture baselines, and excess-property metrics. To support reproducible benchmarking, we curate seven matched pure and mixture physicochemical property datasets. Across multiple mixture-property tasks and model families, we find that strong absolute accuracy can mask poor recovery of non-ideal mixture behavior, and that performance drops substantially under strict molecule splits. These results identify transfer to unseen molecules as a central challenge in molecular mixture machine learning and motivate evaluation beyond absolute accuracy alone.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Behavior-Induced Mirror-Prox Temporal-Difference Learning for Faster Off-Policy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28849)
*Xingguo Chen,Yuchen Shen,Shangdong Yang,Chao Li,Guang Yang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种行为诱导的镜像近端时序差分方法STHTD-MP，通过用行为策略Bellman矩阵的对称部分替换协方差度量来改进混合TD方法的更新几何，在理论上证明了其在标准随机逼近假设下的收敛性，并通过数值实验验证了其相对于GTD2-MP的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有梯度时序差分方法（如Mirror-Prox TD）的性能受辅助变量度量几何的强烈影响，而混合TD方法表明行为策略转移信息能提供更具信息量的更新几何，因此本文旨在设计一种利用行为诱导度量的新方法以提升学习效率。

Method: 提出STHTD-MP方法：在对偶原始-对偶鞍点公式中，用行为策略Bellman矩阵的对称部分替换特征协方差度量；对混合鞍点算子应用Mirror-Prox预测-校正步骤；保持原始变量和辅助变量使用单一学习率。

Result: 理论方面：在标准随机逼近假设下（行为诱导度量正定、联合均值系统Hurwitz、Lyapunov有界性、ODE方法收敛），证明了方法收敛；推导了投影预言机遍历间隙界和与GTD2-MP的精确均值算子比较。实验方面：在two-state、Random Walk和Boyan Chain基准上验证了条件成立，而Baird反例被识别为奇异边界情况。

Conclusion: STHTD-MP在行为诱导度量改善鞍点几何时具有比GTD2-MP更小的均值收缩因子，从而在多个基准任务上表现更优；Baird反例作为奇异边界情况，其严格假设不成立。

Abstract: Gradient temporal-difference methods provide stable off-policy prediction with linear function approximation, but their practical performance is strongly affected by the geometry induced by the auxiliary-variable metric. Existing Mirror-Prox TD methods typically use the feature covariance metric, whereas hybrid TD methods suggest that behavior-policy transition information can provide a more informative update geometry. This paper proposes a behavior-induced Mirror-Prox temporal-difference method, called STHTD-MP, which replaces the covariance metric in the primal-dual saddle-point formulation with the symmetric part of the behavior-policy Bellman matrix. The method keeps a single learning rate for the primal and auxiliary variables and applies a Mirror-Prox prediction-correction step to the resulting hybrid saddle-point operator. We provide a formal convergence analysis for fixed-policy linear prediction under standard stochastic approximation assumptions: the behavior-induced metric is positive definite, the joint mean system is Hurwitz, boundedness follows from a Lyapunov argument, and the stochastic recursion converges by the ODE method. We further derive projected-oracle ergodic gap bounds and an exact mean-operator comparison with GTD2-MP based on the spectral radius of the deterministic Mirror-Prox error matrix. The analysis shows that STHTD-MP can have a smaller mean contraction factor than GTD2-MP when the behavior-induced metric improves the saddle-point geometry. Exact numerical mean-operator analysis on two-state, Random Walk, and Boyan Chain benchmarks supports this condition, while Baird's counterexample is identified as a singular boundary case where the strict assumptions fail.

</details>


### [170] [The Cognitive Categorical Transformer: Category-Theoretic Inductive Biases for Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28864)
*Al Kari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CCT是一个306M参数的语言模型架构，在GPT-2 Small基础上加入范畴论和认知科学启发的组件，通过simplicial message passing在WikiText-103上将困惑度从24.19降至21.27（相对改善12%），消融实验证实84%的提升来自GT-Full组件，并提出了结构/一致性区分的经验模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索能否通过范畴论和认知科学启发的结构化组件（特别是simplicial message passing）来提升预训练语言模型的困惑度表现，并验证哪些类别的归纳偏置对语言建模实际有效。

Method: 在GPT-2 Small（306M参数）基础上，加入基于范畴论的GT-Full（simplicial message passing）、PrecisionWeightedPP等组件，形成CCT架构；采用匹配步数（215K步）、匹配数据、匹配优化器和调度策略的对比协议，在WikiText-103上进行微调和消融实验。

Result: CCT达到21.27验证困惑度，比GPT-2 Small基线（24.19）降低2.92 PPL（12%相对改善）；消融实验表明GT-Full贡献了84%的提升（2.45/2.92 PPL）；一致性类别的先验（sheaf smoothing、adjunction round-trip、curvature regularization）均为负结果。

Conclusion: 提供了首个消融验证的证据，证明simplicial message passing能在306M参数规模下改善语言模型困惑度；提出了结构/一致性区分经验模式：增加新拓扑结构的范畴论先验能改善语言建模，而强制执行一致性恒等式的先验则不能。

Abstract: The Cognitive Categorical Transformer (CCT) is a 306M-parameter architecture that augments a pretrained GPT-2 Small backbone with cognitively grounded components derived from category theory and several inspirations from cognitive science. Under a matched-step protocol (215,000 optimizer steps, matched data, matched optimizer and schedule) on WikiText-103, CCT reaches 21.27 validation perplexity, compared with 24.19 for an identically fine-tuned GPT-2 Small baseline. The architecture therefore contributes a 2.92 PPL (12% relative) reduction beyond what in-domain fine-tuning alone provides. A retrain-from-scratch ablation that holds GT-Full simplicial message passing bypassed across the entire seven-phase activation schedule reaches 23.72 PPL, localizing 84% of the architectural improvement (2.45 of 2.92 PPL) to GT-Full. We present the first ablation-validated evidence that simplicial message passing improves language-model perplexity at the 306M-parameter scale on WikiText-103. Published GPT-2 Large reaches 22.05 zero-shot PPL on WikiText-103 with 6.2x more parameters than GPT-2 Small; this paper treats that number as an external published reference, not as the architectural benchmark. Three negative results on consistency-style categorical priors (sheaf smoothing, adjunction round-trip, curvature regularization) and the joint structural-prior result for GT-Full and PrecisionWeightedPP together support an empirical pattern termed the *structure/consistency distinction*, in which categorical priors that add new topology improve language modeling and those that enforce a consistency identity do not.

</details>


### [171] [Review Arcade: On the Human Alignment and Gameability of LLM Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28897)
*Hans Ole Hatzel,Sebastian Steindl,Jan Strich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本论文实证研究了2025年ACL Rolling Review中LLM生成的论文评审，发现LLM评审与人类评审的一致性有限（最佳情况下尚可），且一致性受提示词和模型影响显著。作者利用LLM评审进行迭代修订（"游戏"评审）可在特定场景下有效提升论文分数，最多35%的论文获得统计显著的分数提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM生成的论文评审在学术界（包括顶级会议官方试点）日益普及，有必要系统评估LLM评审的质量及其潜在影响。同时需防范作者利用LLM评审"投机"修改论文的可能性。

Method: 基于2025年ACL Rolling Review (ARR)的论文数据，从审稿人和作者两个视角进行实证实验：(1) 对比LLM生成评审与人类评审的一致性；(2) 测试不同提示词和模型对一致性的影响；(3) 模拟作者根据LLM评审进行迭代修订的"draft-revise"工作流，评估分数变化。

Result: LLM与人类评审一致性有限，最佳场景下也仅达"合理"水平；一致性随提示词和模型变化显著；作者利用LLM评审迭代修改论文在特定场景下有效，最多35%的论文获得统计显著的总体分数提升。

Conclusion: LLM生成的评审与人类评审存在差距，且容易被"游戏化"利用，这为学术评审的公平性和完整性带来了新的挑战。

Abstract: LLM-generated reviews for scientific papers are gaining considerable traction and are even being officially piloted by major conferences. We have to assume that not only reviewers are using LLM-assistance, but also that authors use LLMs to revise their papers before submitting. In this work, we perform empirical experiments on papers from the 2025 ACL Rolling Review (ARR) to evaluate LLM reviews from both the author and the reviewer perspective. First, we identify a limited alignment of LLM reviews with human ones. In the best-case scenario, the alignment is reasonable. However, we also find that LLM-human alignment varies substantially across prompts and models. Finally, we investigate the scenario in which the author uses an iterative draft-revise workflow to improve the submission according to the LLM review. We find that this "gaming" of LLM reviews can be effective in specific scenarios, leading to a statistically significant increase of overall scores for up to 35\% of papers. We publish our code: https://github.com/uhh-hcds/reviewarcade.

</details>


### [172] [Orthogonal Concept Erasure for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28902)
*Yuhao Sun,Lingyun Yu,Haoxiang Xu,Fengyuan Miao,Zhuoer Xu,Hongtao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出正交概念擦除方法（OCE），通过将编辑型概念擦除从加法参数更新重构为乘法正交变换，解决了现有编辑型方法在精确擦除概念与保持生成能力之间的权衡问题，实现了高效、可扩展的多概念擦除，在4.3秒内可擦除多达100个概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念擦除方法存在明显局限：基于训练的方法计算成本高、可扩展性差；基于编辑的方法虽高效但难以同时实现精确概念擦除和保持整体生成能力。核心问题在于编辑型方法依赖加法参数更新，而加法更新必然纠缠神经元方向、幅度和角度几何，导致概念擦除与生成性能之间产生意外干扰。

Method: 提出正交概念擦除（OCE），将编辑型擦除从几何角度重构为乘法参数更新。具体地，OCE应用层级的正交变换，通过闭式解推导得到参数变换，在实现精确概念擦除的同时保持神经元幅度和角度几何不变。针对多概念擦除中的冲突约束，引入子空间级别目标与结构化子空间操作。

Result: 实验表明，OCE在单概念和多概念擦除任务中均优于现有方法，在保持非目标生成能力的同时实现精确概念擦除，可在4.3秒内擦除多达100个概念。

Conclusion: OCE通过几何视角的乘法正交变换从根本上解决了编辑型概念擦除的固有限制，为扩散模型的安全可控生成提供了一种高效、精确且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Concept erasure has emerged as a promising approach to mitigate undesired or unsafe content in diffusion models, yet existing methods still face significant limitations. While training-based methods are effective, their high computational cost limits scalability. Editing-based methods are more efficient and deployment-friendly, yet they struggle to simultaneously achieve precise concept erasure and preserve overall generative capacity. We identify this core limitation of the editing-based methods as reliance on additive parameter updates. Our empirical analysis reveals that concept semantics primarily depend on neuron direction rather than neuron magnitude, while overall generative capacity relies on the angular geometry of neurons. As additive updates inherently entangle direction, magnitude, and angular geometry, they inevitably introduce unintended interference between concept erasure and overall generation performance. To address this, we propose Orthogonal Concept Erasure (OCE), which reformulates editing-based erasure as multiplicative parameter updates from a geometric perspective. Specifically, OCE applies layer-wise orthogonal transformations derived from a closed-form solution to the parameters, enabling precise concept erasure while preserving the neuron magnitude and angular geometry. Furthermore, to address conflicting constraints in multi-concept erasure, OCE introduces a subspace-level objective with structured subspace manipulation, yielding a more effective and scalable erasure. Extensive experiments on single- and multi-concept erasure demonstrate that OCE outperforms existing methods in concept erasure and non-target preservation, erasing up to 100 concepts in 4.3 s. Code: https://github.com/HansSunY/OCE.

</details>


### [173] [VFEAgent: A Multimodal Agent Framework for End-to-End Automated Finite Element Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28978)
*Jiachen Zhang,Junyi Lao,Chenghao Liu,Siyuan Liu,Shixin Wu,Linsen Zhang,Boyu Wang,Songfang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出VFEAgent，一个端到端的多智能体系统，通过多模态视觉-语言处理和验证优先的代码合成框架，实现从输入图像和问题描述直接自动完成有限元分析建模与仿真，在多项工程力学场景中验证了其高成功率和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限元分析工作流复杂且高度依赖领域专家知识，现有集成大语言模型的方法在处理多模态输入和执行复杂任务方面存在局限性。

Method: VFEAgent包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于ReAct推理的多模态视觉-语言多智能体流水线，从异构输入中提取结构化FEA规范；(2) 验证优先的代码合成框架，包含自调试和回退机制，确保代码可执行性和物理有效性。

Result: VFEAgent在生成完整且物理有效的仿真方面取得了高成功率，在可靠性和正确性上优于基于LLM的基线方法。

Conclusion: 验证了自动化完整FEA工作流的可行性，展示了该框架将工程师从繁琐的手动分析中解放出来的潜力。

Abstract: Finite Element Analysis (FEA) serves as the cornerstone of modern engineering design. However, its workflow is inherently complex and relies heavily on domain expertise. Although recent efforts have integrated Large Language Models (LLMs) into FEA, existing approaches face limitations in handling multimodal inputs and executing complex tasks. To address these limitations, we propose VFEAgent, an end-to-end multi-agent system designed to automate FEA modeling and simulation directly from input images and problem descriptions. Our methodology integrates two core components: (1) a multimodal vision-language multi-agent pipeline that employs ReAct-driven reasoning to extract structured FEA specifications from heterogeneous inputs and (2) a verification-first code synthesis framework, incorporating robust self-debugging and fallback mechanisms to ensure executability and physical validity. We systematically evaluated the system across various engineering mechanics scenarios. The results demonstrate that VFEAgent achieves a high success rate in generating complete and physically valid simulations, outperforming LLM-based baseline methods in reliability and correctness. These findings validate the feasibility of automating the complete FEA workflow, highlighting the framework's potential to liberate engineers from tedious manual analysis.

</details>


### [174] [BEAMS: Benchmarking and Evaluating AI for Modeling and Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28994)
*Sara Metcalf,William Schoenberg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BEAMS倡议通过建立以人为中心的建模与仿真基准，开发开源基础设施和自动化测试，评估AI工具在系统动力学建模中的表现。研究发现AI在讨论和基础定性任务上表现较好，但在因果推理和定量错误修复上较弱，且没有单一LLM在所有引擎类型中占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界决策需要AI工具能够构建仿真模型来支撑其建议并保持可解释性，同时自动化建模工具必须补充而非替代人类专业知识。当前缺乏统一基准来评估AI工具在建模与仿真中的负责任和伦理使用。

Method: BEAMS倡议采用开放的数字化和组织化基础设施，通过协作方式评估AI建模工具。设立指导组确定基准优先级，技术组实施自动化测试。已实现涵盖因果翻译、模型迭代、因果推理、一致性、模型行为解释、建模步骤建议和模型修复建议等类别的评估测试。

Result: 将sd-ai项目引擎与不同LLM组合时，AI工具在讨论和基础定性任务上表现优于因果推理和定量错误修复。没有单一LLM在所有引擎类型中占优，具体任务特性和速度-准确性权衡至关重要。

Conclusion: BEAMS倡议的评估框架有效揭示了不同AI工具在建模支持能力上的差异，未来将纳入关注偏见问题的基准，考虑替代视角和以人为中心的使用场景。

Abstract: AI tools to support real world decision making must be able to build simulation models that inform their recommendations and render them interpretable. Tools that can automate aspects of modeling practice must complement human expertise, not replace it. The BEAMS Initiative aims to guide the development of AI tools for modeling and simulation toward forms that are responsible and ethical by establishing benchmarks for human centered modeling and simulation practices. The initiative uses open digital and organizational infrastructure to collaboratively evaluate AI tools for modeling and simulation. The open source sd ai project hosted by the initiative establishes transparency and enables contributions to be shared broadly. A steering group focuses on prioritizing potential benchmarks, while a technical group focuses on implementing the benchmarks in the form of automated tests. Tests for several distinct categories of evaluation have been implemented and applied to AI tools that support qualitative model building, quantitative model building, and model discussion. These include tests for causal translation, model iteration, causal reasoning, conformance, model behavior explanation, suggested model building steps, and suggested model fixes. When engines from the sd ai project are coupled with different LLMs, their performance on these evaluations reveals variability across different AI tools. The evaluations implemented by the initiative demonstrate that AI enabled modeling tools perform better at discussion and basic qualitative tasks than with causal reasoning and quantitative error fixing. No single LLM dominates across engine types, highlighting the importance of specific tasks and tradeoffs between speed and accuracy. Ongoing efforts of the initiative aim to incorporate benchmarks that address concerns about bias by considering alternative perspectives and human centered use cases.

</details>


### [175] [Adopt $\neq$ Adapt: Longitudinal Analyses of LLM Conversations in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29018)
*Rebecca M. M. Hicke,Kiran Tomlinson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过对约12,000名Bing Copilot用户和WildChat-4.8M数据集的对比分析，发现用户与LLM的交互行为具有高度粘性，个体行为随时间变化微弱；不同活跃度用户差异显著，且WildChat数据集严重偏向"超级用户"，不代表典型用户交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对用户与LLM交互的描述多为静态分析，缺乏对个体用户行为随时间变化的纵向理解，因此需要填补这一空白。

Method: 对约12,000名随机抽样的Microsoft Bing Copilot用户进行对话轨迹分析，并与WildChat-4.8M数据集进行对比。

Result: Copilot数据中存在显著的人群层面趋势，但个体用户轨迹的趋势非常微弱，用户习惯具有极强的粘性；活跃用户对话更成功且任务更复杂专业；WildChat数据集严重偏向高熟练度的"超级用户"。

Conclusion: 现有用户行为难以改变，用户异质性显著；WildChat不代表典型用户-AI交互，这对该数据集的后续使用提出了重要警示。

Abstract: Although a growing body of research has begun to describe user--LLM interactions, the picture it paints is largely static; little is known about how individual users change their behavior over time. To address this gap, we analyze the conversational trajectories of $\sim$12,000 randomly sampled Microsoft Bing Copilot users and compare these with data from WildChat-4.8M. While the Copilot data contains significant population-level trends, we find that trends in individual user trajectories are much weaker; user habits prove to be overwhelmingly sticky. We also find stark differences between users of different activity levels: more active users have more successful conversations and use the LLM for more complex and professionally oriented tasks. Some user trends also appear in WildChat-4.8M, but we find evidence that this dataset is significantly skewed towards highly proficient "power" users. Ultimately, our results suggest that existing user behavior is difficult to change and demonstrate the extent of user heterogeneity. Our comparison between datasets highlights that WildChat does not represent typical user-AI interactions, an important caveat for downstream uses of the data.

</details>


### [176] [When Models Disagree: Rethinking LLM Evaluation for Public Comment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29025)
*Aisha Najera,Alvin Moon,Vedant Srinivasan,Rajesh Veeraraghavan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种可解释性审计管道（Interpretive Audit Pipeline），通过多模型分歧来检测大语言模型在联邦机构公众评论分类中的解释性偏差，发现模型间主题分歧大于提示词变化带来的影响，且专家评分规则会压制但无法解决深层解释分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于立场准确率的评估方法（仅依赖小规模验证集）无法检测不同大语言模型对同一批公众评论产生实质性不同分类结果的问题，需要一种能捕捉解释性复杂性的评估方法。

Method: 构建Interpretive Audit Pipeline：使用四个LLM对1,260条USDA公众评论进行分类，比较模型间分歧与提示词变化的影响；在40条分层子样本上进行两阶段标注（独立标注→看到其他标注者结果后修订），对比人类与模型的修订行为。

Result: 模型间的主题分歧大于同一模型内提示词变化导致的差异；专家评分规则压制了深层解释分歧但未真正解决；人类标注者的修订频繁引入模型集成输出中不存在的框架。

Conclusion: 对于LLM辅助的解释性编码任务，基于分歧的评估是准确率指标的必要补充，而非替代。

Abstract: Federal agencies are deploying large language models (LLMs) to categorize public comment corpora, where the model's organization of the record shapes what policymakers see and which arguments register. Standard evaluation, anchored on stance accuracy against a small validated set, cannot detect when different models produce materially different categorizations of the same public input. We propose an Interpretive Audit Pipeline that treats multi-model disagreement as diagnostic of interpretive complexity and directs human review toward genuinely ambiguous public input. Analyzing 1,260 public comments on a federal USDA docket across four LLMs, we find that inter-model thematic divergence exceeds within-model prompt variation, and that an expert rubric suppresses deep interpretive disagreement without resolving it. In a two-stage labeling study on a stratified 40-comment subsample, four LLMs and a human annotator labeled independently and then revised after seeing the others' labels. Revision behavior varied across labelers, and the human annotator's revisions frequently introduced framings absent from the ensemble's collective output. We argue disagreement-based evaluation is a necessary complement to accuracy metrics for LLM-assisted interpretive coding.

</details>


### [177] [Mind Your Tone: Does Tone Alter LLM Performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29027)
*Om Dobariya,Akhil Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，大型语言模型（LLMs）在客观多项选择题上的准确性会因提示语气的不同而产生系统性变化，这种变化高度依赖模型本身，某些模型受语气影响较小，而另一些则出现显著的准确率波动。研究还揭示了不同学科对语气敏感性的差异，并提出了一种路由框架来解释语气如何影响模型的内部推理模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究提示语气的变化是否以及如何导致LLMs在客观多项选择题上产生不同的准确性表现，从而警示用户在部署LLMs时不应假设其对语气具有鲁棒可靠性。

Method: 使用两个数据集：一个包含50道基础题和5种语气变体，另一个包含570道基础题的MMLU子集（涵盖57个学科）及7种语气变体。对四种经济高效的流行LLM（ChatGPT-4o、ChatGPT-5-nano、Gemini 2.5 Flash和Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite）进行实验评估。

Result: 语气效应具有系统性但高度依赖模型：部分模型仅表现出微小但统计显著的准确率变化，而其他模型在不同语气间则出现大幅准确率波动。此外，不同学科对语气的敏感性存在差异。

Conclusion: 研究结论警示用户，不应假设LLMs在部署时对语气具有鲁棒可靠性。

Abstract: The use of Large Language Models (LLMs) is proliferating, yet their performance is observed to vary based on prompting styles and tones. In this study, we investigate both whether and how tonal variations in prompts lead to disparate LLM accuracy for objective multiple-choice questions. We use two datasets: a 50-base question dataset with five tone variants and a 570-base question MMLU subset spanning 57 subjects with seven tone variants. Experiments were conducted to evaluate the performance of four cost-efficient, popular LLMs: ChatGPT-4o, ChatGPT-5-nano, Gemini 2.5 Flash, and Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite. Across models, tonal effects are systematic but highly model-dependent. Some models show small, yet statistically significant, shifts, while others exhibit large accuracy swings across tones. Further, we identify subject-level differences in tone sensitivity and present a routing framework to explain how tones may attune internal reasoning modes. Our findings caution users against assuming tone-robust reliability in LLM deployments.

</details>


### [178] [Practitioner Beliefs and Behaviors in AI-Enhanced Education: DOT Framework Survey Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29041)
*David Gibson,M. Elizabeth Azukas,Gerald Knezek*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究基于DOT框架（设计思维与开放系统理论），对72名高等教育从业者进行横截面调查，探索AI整合教学中的信念、行为与制度条件。探索性因子分析识别出三维信念结构（AI功能能力、监督治理、教师协作规划），发现从业者认可AI教学支持但强调人工监督，实践中迭代提示和内容生成较多，需求评估与反馈循环较少，且政策、培训、基础设施等制度障碍普遍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对高等教育从业者AI整合教学的系统性实证调查，尤其缺少基于整合性理论框架（如设计思维与开放系统理论）的量化分析，无法全面理解从业者的信念结构、实践行为与制度条件的交互关系。

Method: 采用横截面调查设计（n=72），基于DOT框架编制问卷，涵盖AI熟悉度、使用模式、设计导向实践与教学信念。对19个信念条目进行探索性因子分析（EFA），提取三因子结构（α=.90），并辅以描述性统计报告实践行为与制度障碍。

Result: EFA识别出AI功能能力、监督治理、教师协作规划三因子（α=.90）。从业者普遍认可AI的教学支持价值，但坚持人工监督与批判性评估。实践中迭代提示与内容生成使用频繁，需求评估与反馈循环较少。政策、培训与基础设施等制度性障碍被广泛报告。

Conclusion: DOT框架可作为描述从业者信念与行为的有效模型，但当前实践与设计导向理论间存在差距。研究提供了初步测量结构，未来需开展验证性因子分析与基于结果的研究，将AI支持的设计实践与教学质量相关联。

Abstract: This study reports findings from a cross-sectional survey (n = 72) of higher education practitioners examining beliefs, behaviors, and institutional conditions related to artificial intelligence (AI) integration in teaching and learning. Grounded in the DOT Framework, which integrates design thinking and open systems theory, the study investigates AI familiarity, usage patterns, design-oriented practices, and pedagogical beliefs. Exploratory factor analysis of 19 belief items identified a three-factor structure: AI Functional Capabilities, Oversight and Governance, and Instructor Collaboration and Planning (α = .90). Results indicate that practitioners hold favorable views of AI as a pedagogical support while maintaining strong commitments to human oversight and critical evaluation. Reported practices emphasize iterative prompting and content generation, with less consistent use of needs assessment and feedback loops. Institutional barriers including limited policy, training, and infrastructure were widely reported. These findings provide preliminary empirical support for the DOT Framework as a descriptive model of practitioner beliefs and practices, while also highlighting gaps between design-oriented theory and current implementation. The study contributes an initial measurement structure and identifies directions for confirmatory validation and outcome-based research linking AI-supported design practices to instructional quality.

</details>


### [179] [Differentiable Belief-based Opponent Shaping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29042)
*Aarav G Sane,Karthik Sivachandran,Rohan Paleja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出可微信念对手塑造(D-BOS)，通过微分对手信念更新轨迹来在隐藏角色游戏中实现策略塑造，在混合动机场景中表现优于PPO和BBM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对手塑形方法通常局限于对手的参数、策略或价值空间中进行操作，而隐藏角色游戏中的信念操纵技术又依赖硬编码目标（如欺骗或信念饱和），缺乏端到端可学习的通用框架。

Method: D-BOS将观察者的信念状态视为被塑形的对手状态，通过k步softmax-贝叶斯信念动力学进行微分，实现一阶优化。信念空间公式通过对对手信念更新进行微分来提供塑形信号，并通过聚合多个观察者的个体推断信念轨迹梯度自然扩展到多观察者场景。

Result: 在隐藏角色游戏中，D-BOS在性能上超越PPO和BBM，在混合动机设置中取得了最大的性能提升。

Conclusion: 信念状态可作为对手塑形的有效目标空间，最优策略能够从环境奖励结构中自然涌现，无需显式奖励欺骗或合作行为。

Abstract: Human coordination often relies on the ability to influence the beliefs of others through strategic action. In multi-agent reinforcement learning, opponent shaping attempts to replicate this influence, though existing methods typically operate within an opponent's parameter, policy, or value space. Meanwhile, belief-manipulation techniques in hidden-role games often rely on hard-coded objectives, such as deception or belief saturation. We propose Differentiable Belief-based Opponent Shaping (D-BOS), a first-order method that treats each observer's belief as the shaped opponent state and differentiates through $k$-step softmax-Bayes belief dynamics. Rather than explicitly rewarding deceptive or cooperative behavior, our method treats the belief state as the target for shaping. This allows the optimal strategy to emerge naturally from the environment's reward structure. This belief-space formulation provides an opponent-shaping signal by differentiating through opponent belief updates, and naturally extends to multiple observers by aggregating gradients over their individual inferred belief trajectories. Empirically, D-BOS outperforms PPO and BBM in hidden-role games, with the largest gains in mixed-motive settings.

</details>


### [180] [Hallucination Mitigation with Agentic AI, Nested Learning, and AI Sustainability via Semantic Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29055)
*Diego Gosmar,Deborah A. Dahl*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于HOPE启发式嵌套学习架构、连续记忆系统和语义相似性缓存的混合基准测试方法，通过310条提示语在三级智能体流水线中评估幻觉缓解效果。在五种权重配置下总幻觉评分（THS）降低31.3%-35.9%，语义缓存实现47.3%命中率，将LLM调用次数降至490次。极端可观测性配置获得最负向THS（-0.0709），证明可观测性强化与缓解效果不冲突。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在多智能体流水线中因幻觉（hallucination）导致未支持的声明逐级传播且无法被有效核查，严重制约了生产级部署的可靠性。现有方法缺乏系统的可观测性与缓解机制之间的权衡研究。

Method: 采用HOPE启发的嵌套学习架构，集成连续记忆系统（CMS）和语义相似性缓存；构建310条提示语的混合基准（217条认知不确定性提示+93条捏造诱导压力测试提示）；通过开放地板协议（OFP）编排三级智能体流水线（前端生成器高随机性temperature=1.0，第二级和第三级审核员渐进修正）；定义五项KPI（FCD、FGR、FDF、ECS、OSR）聚合为THS，在五种权重配置下评估缓解-可观测性权衡。

Result: 端到端THS降低31.3%-35.9%；语义缓存440次命中/930次潜在调用（47.3%命中率），LLM调用减至490次；极端可观测性配置获得最负向THS（-0.0709）；在降低能耗和CO2e足迹的同时使多级审核流水线可投入生产规模运行。

Conclusion: 记忆增强的多智能体设计能够在不重新训练模型的情况下同时提升事实可靠性、运行效率和可审计性；可观测性强化配置与幻觉缓解效果相互增益而非折衷。

Abstract: Hallucination remains a major reliability barrier for production LLM systems, particularly in multi-agent pipelines where unsupported claims can propagate unchecked across stages. This paper adapts a HOPE-inspired Nested Learning architecture with Continuum Memory Systems (CMS) and semantic similarity caching to a hybrid benchmark of 310 prompts combining 217 epistemic-uncertainty prompts and 93 fabrication-induction stress-test prompts. A three-stage agentic pipeline orchestrated via the Open Floor Protocol (OFP) is evaluated with five KPIs -- FCD (Factual Claim Density), FGR (Factual Grounding References), FDF (Fictional Disclaimer Frequency), ECS (Explicit Contextualization Score), and OSR (Observability Score Ratio) -- aggregated into THS (Total Hallucination Score) across five weighting configurations to study mitigation-observability trade-offs. FDF, ECS, OSR, and FGR are subtracted as mitigation signals, so that a more negative THS indicates stronger mitigation. The FrontEndAgent is configured as a high-stochasticity generator (temperature = 1.0) to produce a realistic hallucination baseline, while the SecondLevelReviewer and ThirdLevelReviewer operate as progressive correctors. This asymmetric design yields end-to-end THS reductions of -31.3% to -35.9% across five weighting configurations. Semantic caching achieves 440 cache hits over 930 potential calls (47.3% hit rate), reducing LLM invocations to 490, lowering energy and CO2e footprint, and making multi-stage review pipelines operationally viable at production scale. ExtremeObservability attains the most negative final THS (-0.0709), confirming that observability-heavy configurations reinforce rather than compromise mitigation. These findings suggest that memory-augmented multi-agent designs can jointly improve factual reliability, operational efficiency, and auditability without model retraining.

</details>


### [181] [Robust and Efficient Guardrails with Latent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29068)
*Siddharth Sai,Xiaofei Wen,Muhao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COLAGUARD是一种将多步安全推理压缩到连续潜在空间的护栏模型，通过阶段式训练实现推理时直接传播隐藏状态，在匹配显式推理模型性能的同时实现12.9倍加速和22.4倍token消耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于推理的安全护栏虽然性能优于分类基线，但存在高查询延迟和token开销问题，使其难以在高吞吐场景下部署。需要一种既能保持推理能力又能提升推理效率的护栏模型。

Method: 提出COLAGUARD，通过阶段式训练课程（stage-wise training curriculum）将多步安全推理转移到连续潜在空间中，在推理时直接传播隐藏状态（hidden-state propagation），无需生成显式推理链。

Result: 在10个提示和响应审核设置、8个安全基准上，COLAGUARD相较Llama Guard 3提升8.24个宏F1点，匹配GuardReasoner的宏F1性能，同时实现12.9倍速度提升和22.4倍token使用减少。

Conclusion: 潜在推理（latent reasoning）可作为显式推理链生成的可部署替代方案，在提升安全鲁棒性和推理效率之间实现了双赢，而非将二者视为竞争目标。

Abstract: Maintaining the safety of large language models (LLMs) is crucial as they are increasingly deployed in real-world applications. Existing safety guardrails typically rely on single-pass classification or, more recently, distilled reasoning. Reasoning-based guardrails significantly outperform classification-only baselines, but they incur substantial query latency and token overhead that make them impractical for highthroughput deployment. To address this challenge, we propose COLAGUARD, a guardrail model that transfers multi-step safety reasoning into a continuous latent space through a stage-wise training curriculum, enabling direct hidden-state propagation at inference. Evaluated on ten prompt- and response-moderation settings spanning eight safety benchmarks, COLAGUARD improves macro-F1 by 8.24 points over Llama Guard 3 and matches our explicit reasoning baseline, GuardReasoner, in macroF1 while delivering a 12.9X speedup and 22.4X reduction in token usage. Our results suggest that latent reasoning offers a practical alternative to explicit rationale generation for deployable guardrails, jointly improving safety robustness and inference efficiency rather than treating them as competing objectives.

</details>


### [182] [Bridging the Sim-to-Real Gap in Reinforcement Learning-Based Industrial Dispatching through Execution Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29078)
*Jonathan Hoss,Noah Klarmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种策略无关的执行与测量层，用于桥接事件驱动调度策略与工业执行环境之间的语义鸿沟。该层通过构建决策有效快照、定义标准化执行合约并记录策略意图与执行结果之间的 divergence，将不可区分的执行失败转化为结构化、带完整归因的 typed outcomes，从而提升系统可靠性与可解释性。仿真验证表明，该方法在低观测延迟场景下能预防可避免的执行错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件驱动调度策略面临三个关键问题：决策状态时间不一致、动作可采纳性未明确定义、执行错误来源模糊。这些问题限制了系统的可靠性和可解释性，亟需一种中介机制来分离决策语义与执行行为，并使部署失配变得可观测、可归因。

Method: 提出一个策略无关的执行与测量层，核心包括：(1) 从异步事件流中构建决策有效快照；(2) 定义标准化执行合约，明确动作可采纳性；(3) 将执行结果记录为策略意图、事务性结果、物理执行和人工干预之间的 divergence，实现结构化归因。

Result: 通过离散事件仿真评估，该框架在所有观测延迟 regime 下均展现了分析性收益：执行失败从不可区分转化为带完整归因覆盖的结构化 typed outcomes。在低观测延迟场景下操作性收益最为显著，可在提交前预防可避免的执行错误。

Conclusion: 提出的执行与测量层能够将执行不确定性转化为可用于评估和策略优化的监督数据，从根本上提升了事件驱动调度系统的可靠性和可解释性。

Abstract: Event-driven scheduling policies are increasingly deployed in industrial environments, where decisions are made under asynchronous and partially observed system states. As a result, decision states are not temporally consistent, action admissibility is not explicitly defined, and the origin of execution errors remains ambiguous. These issues limit both reliability and interpretability.
  To address this gap, a policy-neutral execution and measurement layer is proposed to mediate between scheduling policies and the industrial execution environment. The layer constructs decision-valid snapshots from asynchronous event streams, defines a standardized execution contract with explicit action admissibility, and records outcomes as divergences between policy intent, transactional outcomes, physical execution, and human intervention. This enables a separation between decision semantics and execution behavior and makes deployment mismatch observable and structurally attributable.
  The proposed framework is evaluated using a discrete-event simulation.
